# Monitor Varlink socket traffic via BPF.
interface io.systemd.VarlinkMonitor

# An object for referencing UNIX processes
type ProcessId(
	# Numeric UNIX PID value
	pid: int,
	# 64bit inode number of pidfd if known
	pidfdId: ?int,
	# Boot ID of the system the inode number belongs to
	bootId: ?string
)

# A filter for matching varlink traffic.
type MonitorFilter(
	# Match traffic where sender or peer has this UID.
	uid: ?int,
	# Match traffic where sender or peer has this PID.
	pid: ?ProcessId,
	# Match traffic on this socket path. Empty string matches sockets without a path.
	path: ?string
)

# Start monitoring varlink traffic with the given filters. Returns a BPF ring buffer fd and keeps the connection open until the client disconnects.
# [Supports 'more' flag]
method Acquire(
	# Capture filters. Each filter's fields are ANDed; the array is ORed. If unset, captures all traffic.
	filters: ?[]MonitorFilter,
	# Controls whether interactive authentication (via polkit) shall be allowed. If unspecified defaults to false.
	allowInteractiveAuthentication: ?bool
) -> (
	# File descriptor index for the BPF ring buffer.
	ringbufFileDescriptor: ?int
)

# A filter parameter is invalid.
error BadFilter(
	parameter: string
)

##### /run/systemd/machine/io.systemd.Machine
interface io.systemd

# Local error if a Varlink connection is disconnected (this never crosses the wire and is synthesized locally only).

# A method call time-out has been reached (also synthesized locally, does not cross wire)
error TimedOut()

error Protocol()

error System(

	# The numeric Linux error number. Typically the name is preferable, if specified.

)

# The method is implemented, but the requested operation is not supported.
error OperationNotSupported()


interface io.systemd.Machine

# The class of a machine
type MachineClass(
	container,
	vm,
	host
)

# What to send a signal to in a machine
type KillWhom(
	leader,
	supervisor,
	all
)

# A enum field allowing to gracefully get metadata
type AcquireMetadata(
	# Do not include metadata in the output
	no,
	# Include metadata in the output
	yes,
	# Include metadata in the output, but gracefully eat up errors
	graceful
)

# A enum field which defines way to open TTY for a machine
type MachineOpenMode(
	# This mode allocates a pseudo TTY in the container and returns a file descriptor and its path. This is equivalent to transitioning into the container and invoking posix_openpt(3).
	tty,
	# This mode allocates a pseudo TTY in the container and ensures that a getty login prompt of the container is running on the other end. It returns the file descriptor of the PTY and the PTY path. This is useful for acquiring a pty with a login prompt from the container.
	login,
	# This mode allocates a pseudo TTY in the container, as the specified user, and invokes the executable at the specified path with a list of arguments (starting from argv[0]) and an environment block. It then returns the file descriptor of the PTY and the PTY path.
	shell
)

# An object for referencing UNIX processes
type ProcessId(
	# Numeric UNIX PID value
	pid: int,
	# 64bit inode number of pidfd if known
	pidfdId: ?int,
	# Boot ID of the system the inode number belongs to
	bootId: ?string
)

# A timestamp object consisting of both CLOCK_REALTIME and CLOCK_MONOTONIC timestamps
type Timestamp(
	# Timestamp in µs in the CLOCK_REALTIME clock (wallclock)
	realtime: ?int,
	# Timestamp in µs in the CLOCK_MONOTONIC clock
	monotonic: ?int
)

# An address object
type Address(
	ifindex: ?int,
	family: int,
	address: []int
)

method Register(
	name: string,
	id: ?string,
	service: ?string,
	class: MachineClass,
	# The leader PID as simple positive integer.
	leader: ?int,
	# The leader PID as ProcessId structure. If both the leader and leaderProcessId parameters are specified they must reference the same process. Typically one would only specify one or the other however. It's generally recommended to specify leaderProcessId as it references a process in a robust way without risk of identifier recycling.
	leaderProcessId: ?ProcessId,
	# The supervisor PID as simple positive integer. Deprecated and ignored: the supervisor is now always derived from the caller's socket (kept for backward compatibility).
	supervisor: ?int,
	# The supervisor PID as ProcessId structure. Deprecated and ignored: the supervisor is now always derived from the caller's socket (kept for backward compatibility).
	supervisorProcessId: ?ProcessId,
	rootDirectory: ?string,
	ifIndices: ?[]int,
	vSockCid: ?int,
	sshAddress: ?string,
	sshPrivateKeyPath: ?string,
	# Varlink socket address for direct machine control. The server at this address is expected to implement io.systemd.MachineInstance and optionally io.systemd.VirtualMachineInstance.
	controlAddress: ?string,
	# Controls whether to allocate a scope unit for the machine to register. If false, the client already took care of that and registered a service/scope specific to the machine.
	allocateUnit: ?bool,
	# Controls whether interactive authentication (via polkit) shall be allowed. If unspecified defaults to false.
	allowInteractiveAuthentication: ?bool
) -> ()

method Unregister(
	# If non-null the name of a machine.
	name: ?string,
	# If non-null the PID of a machine. Special value 0 means to take pid of the machine the caller is part of.
	pid: ?ProcessId,
	# Controls whether interactive authentication (via polkit) shall be allowed. If unspecified defaults to false.
	allowInteractiveAuthentication: ?bool
) -> ()

# Terminate machine, killing its processes
method Terminate(
	# If non-null the name of a machine.
	name: ?string,
	# If non-null the PID of a machine. Special value 0 means to take pid of the machine the caller is part of.
	pid: ?ProcessId,
	# Controls whether interactive authentication (via polkit) shall be allowed. If unspecified defaults to false.
	allowInteractiveAuthentication: ?bool
) -> ()

# Send a UNIX signal to the machine's processes
method Kill(
	# If non-null the name of a machine.
	name: ?string,
	# If non-null the PID of a machine. Special value 0 means to take pid of the machine the caller is part of.
	pid: ?ProcessId,
	# Controls whether interactive authentication (via polkit) shall be allowed. If unspecified defaults to false.
	allowInteractiveAuthentication: ?bool,
	# Identifier that specifies what precisely to send the signal to (either 'leader', 'supervisor', or 'all').
	whom: ?KillWhom,
	# Numeric UNIX signal integer.
	signal: int
) -> ()

# List running machines
# [Supports 'more' flag]
method List(
	# If non-null the name of a machine.
	name: ?string,
	# If non-null the PID of a machine. Special value 0 means to take pid of the machine the caller is part of.
	pid: ?ProcessId,
	# Controls whether interactive authentication (via polkit) shall be allowed. If unspecified defaults to false.
	allowInteractiveAuthentication: ?bool,
	# If 'yes' the output will include machine metadata fields such as 'Addresses', 'OSRelease', and 'UIDShift'. If 'graceful' it's equal to true but gracefully eats up errors
	acquireMetadata: ?AcquireMetadata
) -> (
	# Name of the machine
	name: string,
	# 128bit ID identifying this machine, formatted in hexadecimal
	id: ?string,
	# Name of the software that registered this machine
	service: ?string,
	# The class of this machine
	class: MachineClass,
	# Leader process PID of this machine
	leader: ?ProcessId,
	# Supervisor process PID of this machine
	supervisor: ?ProcessId,
	# Root directory of this machine, if known, relative to host file system
	rootDirectory: ?string,
	# The service manager unit this machine resides in
	unit: ?string,
	# Timestamp when the machine was activated
	timestamp: ?Timestamp,
	# AF_VSOCK CID of the machine if known and applicable
	vSockCid: ?int,
	# SSH address to connect to
	sshAddress: ?string,
	# Path to private SSH key
	sshPrivateKeyPath: ?string,
	# Varlink socket address for direct machine control, implementing io.systemd.MachineInstance and optionally further interfaces
	controlAddress: ?string,
	# List of addresses of the machine
	addresses: ?[]Address,
	# OS release information of the machine. It contains an array of key value pairs read from the os-release(5) file in the image.
	OSRelease: ?[]string,
	# Return the base UID/GID of the machine
	UIDShift: ?int,
	# Subcgroup path of the machine, relative to the unit's cgroup path
	subgroup: ?string,
	# Numeric UNIX UID of the user who registered the machine
	UID: ?int
)

# Allocates a pseudo TTY in the container in various modes
method Open(
	# If non-null the name of a machine.
	name: ?string,
	# If non-null the PID of a machine. Special value 0 means to take pid of the machine the caller is part of.
	pid: ?ProcessId,
	# Controls whether interactive authentication (via polkit) shall be allowed. If unspecified defaults to false.
	allowInteractiveAuthentication: ?bool,
	# There are three possible values: 'tty', 'login', and 'shell'. Please see description for each of the modes.
	mode: MachineOpenMode,
	# See description of mode='shell'. Valid only when mode='shell'
	user: ?string,
	# See description of mode='shell'. Valid only when mode='shell'
	path: ?string,
	# See description of mode='shell'. Valid only when mode='shell'
	args: ?[]string,
	# See description of mode='shell'. Valid only when mode='shell'
	environment: ?[]string
) -> (
	# File descriptor of the allocated pseudo TTY
	ptyFileDescriptor: int,
	# Path to the allocated pseudo TTY
	ptyPath: string
)

# Maps given machine's UID/GID to host's UID/GID
method MapFrom(
	# If non-null the name of a machine.
	name: ?string,
	# If non-null the PID of a machine. Special value 0 means to take pid of the machine the caller is part of.
	pid: ?ProcessId,
	# Controls whether interactive authentication (via polkit) shall be allowed. If unspecified defaults to false.
	allowInteractiveAuthentication: ?bool,
	# UID in the machine to map to host UID
	uid: ?int,
	# GID in the machine to map to host GID
	gid: ?int
) -> (
	# Mapped UID
	uid: ?int,
	# Mapped GID
	gid: ?int
)

# Maps given host's UID/GID to a machine and corresponding UID/GID
method MapTo(
	# Host UID to map to machine UID
	uid: ?int,
	# Host GID to map to machine GID
	gid: ?int
) -> (
	# Mapped UID
	uid: ?int,
	# Mapped GID
	gid: ?int,
	# Machine's name which owns mapped UID/GID
	machineName: ?string
)

# Bind mounts a file or directory from the host into the container
method BindMount(
	# If non-null the name of a machine.
	name: ?string,
	# If non-null the PID of a machine. Special value 0 means to take pid of the machine the caller is part of.
	pid: ?ProcessId,
	# Controls whether interactive authentication (via polkit) shall be allowed. If unspecified defaults to false.
	allowInteractiveAuthentication: ?bool,
	# The source directory/file on the host. The path must be absolute.
	source: string,
	# The destination directory/file in the container. The path must be absolute. If null, it's equal to 'source'
	destination: ?string,
	# If true, the bind mount shall be read-only
	readOnly: ?bool,
	# The destination mount point shall be created first, if it is missing
	mkdir: ?bool
) -> ()

# Copy files or directories from a container into the host
method CopyFrom(
	# If non-null the name of a machine.
	name: ?string,
	# If non-null the PID of a machine. Special value 0 means to take pid of the machine the caller is part of.
	pid: ?ProcessId,
	# Controls whether interactive authentication (via polkit) shall be allowed. If unspecified defaults to false.
	allowInteractiveAuthentication: ?bool,
	# The absolute path to source directory/file in the container
	source: string,
	# The absolute path to destination directory/file on the host. If null, it's equal to 'source'.
	destination: ?string,
	# If true the destination will be replaced
	replace: ?bool
) -> ()

# Copy files or directories from the host into a container
method CopyTo(
	# If non-null the name of a machine.
	name: ?string,
	# If non-null the PID of a machine. Special value 0 means to take pid of the machine the caller is part of.
	pid: ?ProcessId,
	# Controls whether interactive authentication (via polkit) shall be allowed. If unspecified defaults to false.
	allowInteractiveAuthentication: ?bool,
	# The absolute path to source directory/file on the host
	source: string,
	# The absolute path to destination directory/file in the container. If null, it's equal to 'source'
	destination: ?string,
	# If true the destination will be replaced
	replace: ?bool
) -> ()

# Opens machine's root directory
method OpenRootDirectory(
	# If non-null the name of a machine.
	name: ?string,
	# If non-null the PID of a machine. Special value 0 means to take pid of the machine the caller is part of.
	pid: ?ProcessId,
	# Controls whether interactive authentication (via polkit) shall be allowed. If unspecified defaults to false.
	allowInteractiveAuthentication: ?bool
) -> (
	# File descriptor of opened root directory
	fileDescriptor: int
)

# No matching machine currently running
error NoSuchMachine()

error MachineExists()

# Machine does not use private networking
error NoPrivateNetworking()

# Machine does not contain OS release information
error NoOSReleaseInformation()

# Machine uses a complex UID/GID mapping, cannot determine shift
error NoUIDShift()

# Requested information is not available
error NotAvailable()

# Requested operation is not supported
error NotSupported()

# There is no IPC service (such as system bus or varlink) in the container
error NoIPC()

# No such user
error NoSuchUser()

# No such group
error NoSuchGroup()

# User belongs to host UID range
error UserInHostRange()

# Group belongs to host GID range
error GroupInHostRange()


interface io.systemd.MachineImage

# A enum field allowing to gracefully get metadata
type AcquireMetadata(
	# Do not include metadata in the output
	no,
	# Include metadata in the output
	yes,
	# Include metadata in the output, but gracefully eat up errors
	graceful
)

# A enum field allowing to control what type of images are cleaned up
type CleanPoolMode(
	# Remove all unused images
	all,
	# Remove only hidden images
	hidden
)

# List images
# [Supports 'more' flag]
method List(
	# If non-null the name of a image to report details on.
	name: ?string,
	# If true the output will include image metadata fields such as 'machineInfo' and 'OSRelease'.
	acquireMetadata: ?AcquireMetadata,
	# Controls whether interactive authentication (via polkit) shall be allowed. If unspecified defaults to false.
	allowInteractiveAuthentication: ?bool
) -> (
	# Name of the image
	name: string,
	# The file system path where image is stored
	path: ?string,
	# The type of this image
	type: string,
	# The class of this image
	class: string,
	# Whether the image is read-only
	readOnly: bool,
	# The image creation timestamp
	creationTimestamp: ?int,
	# The image creation timestamp
	modificationTimestamp: ?int,
	# The image creation timestamp
	usage: ?int,
	# The image disk usage (exclusive)
	usageExclusive: ?int,
	# The image disk usage (exclusive)
	limit: ?int,
	# The image disk usage limit (exclusive)
	limitExclusive: ?int,
	# The hostname of the image
	hostname: ?string,
	# The machine ID of the image
	machineId: ?string,
	# Machine info information of an image. It contains an array of key value pairs read from the machine-info(5) file in the image.
	machineInfo: ?[]string,
	# OS release information of an image. It contains an array of key value pairs read from the os-release(5) file in the image.
	OSRelease: ?[]string
)

# Update image allowing to rename, toggle read-only flag, or set a limit
method Update(
	# The name of an image
	name: string,
	# Controls whether interactive authentication (via polkit) shall be allowed. If unspecified defaults to false.
	allowInteractiveAuthentication: ?bool,
	# If non-null the new name of the image
	newName: ?string,
	# If non-null value of the read-only flag of the image
	readOnly: ?bool,
	# If non-null value of image quota limit
	limit: ?int
) -> ()

# Clone image
method Clone(
	# The name of an image
	name: string,
	# Controls whether interactive authentication (via polkit) shall be allowed. If unspecified defaults to false.
	allowInteractiveAuthentication: ?bool,
	# The new name of the image
	newName: string,
	# If non-null value of the read-only flag of the image
	readOnly: ?bool
) -> ()

# Remove image
method Remove(
	# The name of an image
	name: string,
	# Controls whether interactive authentication (via polkit) shall be allowed. If unspecified defaults to false.
	allowInteractiveAuthentication: ?bool
) -> ()

# Sets an overall quota limit on the pool of images
method SetPoolLimit(
	# Controls whether interactive authentication (via polkit) shall be allowed. If unspecified defaults to false.
	allowInteractiveAuthentication: ?bool,
	# New image quota limit
	limit: int
) -> ()

# Clean unused images depending on the specified mode. Note that it does not process vendon and host images.
# [Requires 'more' flag]
method CleanPool(
	# Controls whether interactive authentication (via polkit) shall be allowed. If unspecified defaults to false.
	allowInteractiveAuthentication: ?bool,
	# Allows removing all or only hidden images
	mode: CleanPoolMode
) -> (
	# Image name
	name: string,
	# The image disk usage (exclusive)
	usageExclusive: ?int
)

# No matching image exists
error NoSuchImage()

# Too many ongoing background operations
error TooManyOperations()

# Requested operation is not supported
error NotSupported()


# An interface to control basic properties of systemd services.
interface io.systemd.service

# Checks if the service is running.
method Ping() -> ()

# Reloads configuration files.
method Reload(
	# Controls whether interactive authentication (via polkit) shall be allowed. If unspecified defaults to false.
	allowInteractiveAuthentication: ?bool
) -> ()

# Sets the maximum log level.
method SetLogLevel(
	# The maximum log level, using BSD syslog log level integers.
	level: ?int
) -> ()

# Gets the maximum log level.
method GetLogLevel() -> (
	# The current maximum log level, using BSD syslog level integers.
	level: int
)

# Get current environment block.
method GetEnvironment() -> (
	# Returns the current environment block, i.e. the contents of environ[].
	environment: ?[]string
)

# Returned if the environment block is currently not in a valid state.
error InconsistentEnvironment()


# General Varlink service interface
interface org.varlink.service

# Get service meta information
method GetInfo() -> (
	# String identifying the vendor of this service
	vendor: string,
	# String identifying the product implementing this service
	product: string,
	# Version string of this product
	version: string,
	# Web URL pointing to additional information about this service
	url: string,
	# List of interfaces implemented by this service
	interfaces: []string
)

# Get description of an implemented interface in Varlink IDL format
method GetInterfaceDescription(
	# Name of interface to query interface description of
	interface: string
) -> (
	# Interface description in Varlink IDL format
	description: string
)

# Error returned if a method is called on an unknown interface
error InterfaceNotFound(
	# Name of interface that was called but does not exist
	interface: string
)

# Error returned if an unknown method is called on an known interface
error MethodNotFound(
	# Name of method that was called but does not exist
	method: string
)

# Error returned if a method is called that is known but not implemented
error MethodNotImplemented(
	# Name of method that was called but is not implemented
	method: string
)

# Error returned if a method is called with an invalid parameter
error InvalidParameter(
	# Name of the invalid parameter
	parameter: string
)

# General permission error
error PermissionDenied()

# A method was called with the 'more' flag off, but it may only be called with the flag turned on
error ExpectedMore()
##### /run/systemd/machine/io.systemd.MachineImage
interface io.systemd

# Local error if a Varlink connection is disconnected (this never crosses the wire and is synthesized locally only).
error Disconnected()

# A method call time-out has been reached (also synthesized locally, does not cross wire)
error TimedOut()

# Some form of protocol error (also synthesized locally, does not cross wire)
error Protocol()

# A generic Linux system error ("errno"s).
error System(
	# The origin of this system error, typically 'linux' to indicate Linux error numbers.
	origin: ?string,
	# The Linux error name, i.e. ENOENT, EHWPOISON or similar.
	errnoName: ?string,
	# The numeric Linux error number. Typically the name is preferable, if specified.
	errno: ?int
)

# The method is implemented, but the requested operation is not supported.
error OperationNotSupported()


interface io.systemd.Machine

# The class of a machine
type MachineClass(
	container,
	vm,
	host
)

# What to send a signal to in a machine
type KillWhom(
	leader,
	supervisor,
	all
)

# A enum field allowing to gracefully get metadata
type AcquireMetadata(
	# Do not include metadata in the output
	no,
	# Include metadata in the output
	yes,
	# Include metadata in the output, but gracefully eat up errors
	graceful
)

# A enum field which defines way to open TTY for a machine
type MachineOpenMode(
	# This mode allocates a pseudo TTY in the container and returns a file descriptor and its path. This is equivalent to transitioning into the container and invoking posix_openpt(3).
	tty,
	# This mode allocates a pseudo TTY in the container and ensures that a getty login prompt of the container is running on the other end. It returns the file descriptor of the PTY and the PTY path. This is useful for acquiring a pty with a login prompt from the container.
	login,
	# This mode allocates a pseudo TTY in the container, as the specified user, and invokes the executable at the specified path with a list of arguments (starting from argv[0]) and an environment block. It then returns the file descriptor of the PTY and the PTY path.
	shell
)

# An object for referencing UNIX processes
type ProcessId(
	# Numeric UNIX PID value
	pid: int,
	# 64bit inode number of pidfd if known
	pidfdId: ?int,
	# Boot ID of the system the inode number belongs to
	bootId: ?string
)

# A timestamp object consisting of both CLOCK_REALTIME and CLOCK_MONOTONIC timestamps
type Timestamp(
	# Timestamp in µs in the CLOCK_REALTIME clock (wallclock)
	realtime: ?int,
	# Timestamp in µs in the CLOCK_MONOTONIC clock
	monotonic: ?int
)

# An address object
type Address(
	ifindex: ?int,
	family: int,
	address: []int
)

method Register(
	name: string,
	id: ?string,
	service: ?string,
	class: MachineClass,
	# The leader PID as simple positive integer.
	leader: ?int,
	# The leader PID as ProcessId structure. If both the leader and leaderProcessId parameters are specified they must reference the same process. Typically one would only specify one or the other however. It's generally recommended to specify leaderProcessId as it references a process in a robust way without risk of identifier recycling.
	leaderProcessId: ?ProcessId,
	# The supervisor PID as simple positive integer. Deprecated and ignored: the supervisor is now always derived from the caller's socket (kept for backward compatibility).
	supervisor: ?int,
	# The supervisor PID as ProcessId structure. Deprecated and ignored: the supervisor is now always derived from the caller's socket (kept for backward compatibility).
	supervisorProcessId: ?ProcessId,
	rootDirectory: ?string,
	ifIndices: ?[]int,
	vSockCid: ?int,
	sshAddress: ?string,
	sshPrivateKeyPath: ?string,
	# Varlink socket address for direct machine control. The server at this address is expected to implement io.systemd.MachineInstance and optionally io.systemd.VirtualMachineInstance.
	controlAddress: ?string,
	# Controls whether to allocate a scope unit for the machine to register. If false, the client already took care of that and registered a service/scope specific to the machine.
	allocateUnit: ?bool,
	# Controls whether interactive authentication (via polkit) shall be allowed. If unspecified defaults to false.
	allowInteractiveAuthentication: ?bool
) -> ()

method Unregister(
	# If non-null the name of a machine.
	name: ?string,
	# If non-null the PID of a machine. Special value 0 means to take pid of the machine the caller is part of.
	pid: ?ProcessId,
	# Controls whether interactive authentication (via polkit) shall be allowed. If unspecified defaults to false.
	allowInteractiveAuthentication: ?bool
) -> ()

# Terminate machine, killing its processes
method Terminate(
	# If non-null the name of a machine.
	name: ?string,
	# If non-null the PID of a machine. Special value 0 means to take pid of the machine the caller is part of.
	pid: ?ProcessId,
	# Controls whether interactive authentication (via polkit) shall be allowed. If unspecified defaults to false.
	allowInteractiveAuthentication: ?bool
) -> ()

# Send a UNIX signal to the machine's processes
method Kill(
	# If non-null the name of a machine.
	name: ?string,
	# If non-null the PID of a machine. Special value 0 means to take pid of the machine the caller is part of.
	pid: ?ProcessId,
	# Controls whether interactive authentication (via polkit) shall be allowed. If unspecified defaults to false.
	allowInteractiveAuthentication: ?bool,
	# Identifier that specifies what precisely to send the signal to (either 'leader', 'supervisor', or 'all').
	whom: ?KillWhom,
	# Numeric UNIX signal integer.
	signal: int
) -> ()

# List running machines
# [Supports 'more' flag]
method List(
	# If non-null the name of a machine.
	name: ?string,
	# If non-null the PID of a machine. Special value 0 means to take pid of the machine the caller is part of.
	pid: ?ProcessId,
	# Controls whether interactive authentication (via polkit) shall be allowed. If unspecified defaults to false.
	allowInteractiveAuthentication: ?bool,
	# If 'yes' the output will include machine metadata fields such as 'Addresses', 'OSRelease', and 'UIDShift'. If 'graceful' it's equal to true but gracefully eats up errors
	acquireMetadata: ?AcquireMetadata
) -> (
	# Name of the machine
	name: string,
	# 128bit ID identifying this machine, formatted in hexadecimal
	id: ?string,
	# Name of the software that registered this machine
	service: ?string,
	# The class of this machine
	class: MachineClass,
	# Leader process PID of this machine
	leader: ?ProcessId,
	# Supervisor process PID of this machine
	supervisor: ?ProcessId,
	# Root directory of this machine, if known, relative to host file system
	rootDirectory: ?string,
	# The service manager unit this machine resides in
	unit: ?string,
	# Timestamp when the machine was activated
	timestamp: ?Timestamp,
	# AF_VSOCK CID of the machine if known and applicable
	vSockCid: ?int,
	# SSH address to connect to
	sshAddress: ?string,
	# Path to private SSH key
	sshPrivateKeyPath: ?string,
	# Varlink socket address for direct machine control, implementing io.systemd.MachineInstance and optionally further interfaces
	controlAddress: ?string,
	# List of addresses of the machine
	addresses: ?[]Address,
	# OS release information of the machine. It contains an array of key value pairs read from the os-release(5) file in the image.
	OSRelease: ?[]string,
	# Return the base UID/GID of the machine
	UIDShift: ?int,
	# Subcgroup path of the machine, relative to the unit's cgroup path
	subgroup: ?string,
	# Numeric UNIX UID of the user who registered the machine
	UID: ?int
)

# Allocates a pseudo TTY in the container in various modes
method Open(
	# If non-null the name of a machine.
	name: ?string,
	# If non-null the PID of a machine. Special value 0 means to take pid of the machine the caller is part of.
	pid: ?ProcessId,
	# Controls whether interactive authentication (via polkit) shall be allowed. If unspecified defaults to false.
	allowInteractiveAuthentication: ?bool,
	# There are three possible values: 'tty', 'login', and 'shell'. Please see description for each of the modes.
	mode: MachineOpenMode,
	# See description of mode='shell'. Valid only when mode='shell'
	user: ?string,
	# See description of mode='shell'. Valid only when mode='shell'
	path: ?string,
	# See description of mode='shell'. Valid only when mode='shell'
	args: ?[]string,
	# See description of mode='shell'. Valid only when mode='shell'
	environment: ?[]string
) -> (
	# File descriptor of the allocated pseudo TTY
	ptyFileDescriptor: int,
	# Path to the allocated pseudo TTY
	ptyPath: string
)

# Maps given machine's UID/GID to host's UID/GID
method MapFrom(
	# If non-null the name of a machine.
	name: ?string,
	# If non-null the PID of a machine. Special value 0 means to take pid of the machine the caller is part of.
	pid: ?ProcessId,
	# Controls whether interactive authentication (via polkit) shall be allowed. If unspecified defaults to false.
	allowInteractiveAuthentication: ?bool,
	# UID in the machine to map to host UID
	uid: ?int,
	# GID in the machine to map to host GID
	gid: ?int
) -> (
	# Mapped UID
	uid: ?int,
	# Mapped GID
	gid: ?int
)

# Maps given host's UID/GID to a machine and corresponding UID/GID
method MapTo(
	# Host UID to map to machine UID
	uid: ?int,
	# Host GID to map to machine GID
	gid: ?int
) -> (
	# Mapped UID
	uid: ?int,
	# Mapped GID
	gid: ?int,
	# Machine's name which owns mapped UID/GID
	machineName: ?string
)

# Bind mounts a file or directory from the host into the container
method BindMount(
	# If non-null the name of a machine.
	name: ?string,
	# If non-null the PID of a machine. Special value 0 means to take pid of the machine the caller is part of.
	pid: ?ProcessId,
	# Controls whether interactive authentication (via polkit) shall be allowed. If unspecified defaults to false.
	allowInteractiveAuthentication: ?bool,
	# The source directory/file on the host. The path must be absolute.
	source: string,
	# The destination directory/file in the container. The path must be absolute. If null, it's equal to 'source'
	destination: ?string,
	# If true, the bind mount shall be read-only
	readOnly: ?bool,
	# The destination mount point shall be created first, if it is missing
	mkdir: ?bool
) -> ()

# Copy files or directories from a container into the host
method CopyFrom(
	# If non-null the name of a machine.
	name: ?string,
	# If non-null the PID of a machine. Special value 0 means to take pid of the machine the caller is part of.
	pid: ?ProcessId,
	# Controls whether interactive authentication (via polkit) shall be allowed. If unspecified defaults to false.
	allowInteractiveAuthentication: ?bool,
	# The absolute path to source directory/file in the container
	source: string,
	# The absolute path to destination directory/file on the host. If null, it's equal to 'source'.
	destination: ?string,
	# If true the destination will be replaced
	replace: ?bool
) -> ()

# Copy files or directories from the host into a container
method CopyTo(
	# If non-null the name of a machine.
	name: ?string,
	# If non-null the PID of a machine. Special value 0 means to take pid of the machine the caller is part of.
	pid: ?ProcessId,
	# Controls whether interactive authentication (via polkit) shall be allowed. If unspecified defaults to false.
	allowInteractiveAuthentication: ?bool,
	# The absolute path to source directory/file on the host
	source: string,
	# The absolute path to destination directory/file in the container. If null, it's equal to 'source'
	destination: ?string,
	# If true the destination will be replaced
	replace: ?bool
) -> ()

# Opens machine's root directory
method OpenRootDirectory(
	# If non-null the name of a machine.
	name: ?string,
	# If non-null the PID of a machine. Special value 0 means to take pid of the machine the caller is part of.
	pid: ?ProcessId,
	# Controls whether interactive authentication (via polkit) shall be allowed. If unspecified defaults to false.
	allowInteractiveAuthentication: ?bool
) -> (
	# File descriptor of opened root directory
	fileDescriptor: int
)

# No matching machine currently running
error NoSuchMachine()

error MachineExists()

# Machine does not use private networking
error NoPrivateNetworking()

# Machine does not contain OS release information
error NoOSReleaseInformation()

# Machine uses a complex UID/GID mapping, cannot determine shift
error NoUIDShift()

# Requested information is not available
error NotAvailable()

# Requested operation is not supported
error NotSupported()

# There is no IPC service (such as system bus or varlink) in the container
error NoIPC()

# No such user
error NoSuchUser()

# No such group
error NoSuchGroup()

# User belongs to host UID range
error UserInHostRange()

# Group belongs to host GID range
error GroupInHostRange()


interface io.systemd.MachineImage

# A enum field allowing to gracefully get metadata
type AcquireMetadata(
	# Do not include metadata in the output
	no,
	# Include metadata in the output
	yes,
	# Include metadata in the output, but gracefully eat up errors
	graceful
)

# A enum field allowing to control what type of images are cleaned up
type CleanPoolMode(
	# Remove all unused images
	all,
	# Remove only hidden images
	hidden
)

# List images
# [Supports 'more' flag]
method List(
	# If non-null the name of a image to report details on.
	name: ?string,
	# If true the output will include image metadata fields such as 'machineInfo' and 'OSRelease'.
	acquireMetadata: ?AcquireMetadata,
	# Controls whether interactive authentication (via polkit) shall be allowed. If unspecified defaults to false.
	allowInteractiveAuthentication: ?bool
) -> (
	# Name of the image
	name: string,
	# The file system path where image is stored
	path: ?string,
	# The type of this image
	type: string,
	# The class of this image
	class: string,
	# Whether the image is read-only
	readOnly: bool,
	# The image creation timestamp
	creationTimestamp: ?int,
	# The image creation timestamp
	modificationTimestamp: ?int,
	# The image creation timestamp
	usage: ?int,
	# The image disk usage (exclusive)
	usageExclusive: ?int,
	# The image disk usage (exclusive)
	limit: ?int,
	# The image disk usage limit (exclusive)
	limitExclusive: ?int,
	# The hostname of the image
	hostname: ?string,
	# The machine ID of the image
	machineId: ?string,
	# Machine info information of an image. It contains an array of key value pairs read from the machine-info(5) file in the image.
	machineInfo: ?[]string,
	# OS release information of an image. It contains an array of key value pairs read from the os-release(5) file in the image.
	OSRelease: ?[]string
)

# Update image allowing to rename, toggle read-only flag, or set a limit
method Update(
	# The name of an image
	name: string,
	# Controls whether interactive authentication (via polkit) shall be allowed. If unspecified defaults to false.
	allowInteractiveAuthentication: ?bool,
	# If non-null the new name of the image
	newName: ?string,
	# If non-null value of the read-only flag of the image
	readOnly: ?bool,
	# If non-null value of image quota limit
	limit: ?int
) -> ()

# Clone image
method Clone(
	# The name of an image
	name: string,
	# Controls whether interactive authentication (via polkit) shall be allowed. If unspecified defaults to false.
	allowInteractiveAuthentication: ?bool,
	# The new name of the image
	newName: string,
	# If non-null value of the read-only flag of the image
	readOnly: ?bool
) -> ()

# Remove image
method Remove(
	# The name of an image
	name: string,
	# Controls whether interactive authentication (via polkit) shall be allowed. If unspecified defaults to false.
	allowInteractiveAuthentication: ?bool
) -> ()

# Sets an overall quota limit on the pool of images
method SetPoolLimit(
	# Controls whether interactive authentication (via polkit) shall be allowed. If unspecified defaults to false.
	allowInteractiveAuthentication: ?bool,
	# New image quota limit
	limit: int
) -> ()

# Clean unused images depending on the specified mode. Note that it does not process vendon and host images.
# [Requires 'more' flag]
method CleanPool(
	# Controls whether interactive authentication (via polkit) shall be allowed. If unspecified defaults to false.
	allowInteractiveAuthentication: ?bool,
	# Allows removing all or only hidden images
	mode: CleanPoolMode
) -> (
	# Image name
	name: string,
	# The image disk usage (exclusive)
	usageExclusive: ?int
)

# No matching image exists
error NoSuchImage()

# Too many ongoing background operations
error TooManyOperations()

# Requested operation is not supported
error NotSupported()

# Journal control APIs
interface io.systemd.Journal

# Write out all pending log messages out to disk, and reply only after that's complete.
method Synchronize(
	# Controls whether to offline the journal files as part of the synchronization operation.
	offline: ?bool
) -> ()

# Rotate journal files, i.e. close existing files, start new ones.
method Rotate() -> ()

# Flush runtime logs to persistent logs, i.e. flush log data from /run/ into /var/, and continue writing future log data to the latter location.
method FlushToVar() -> ()

# Relinquish use of /var/ again, return to do runtime logging into /run/ only.
method RelinquishVar() -> ()

# Journal service running as per-namespace instance, and requested operation is not supported for namespaced journal.
error NotSupportedByNamespaces()

# General Varlink service interface
interface org.varlink.service

# Get service meta information
method GetInfo() -> (
	# String identifying the vendor of this service
	vendor: string,
	# String identifying the product implementing this service
	product: string,
	# Version string of this product
	version: string,
	# Web URL pointing to additional information about this service
	url: string,
	# List of interfaces implemented by this service
	interfaces: []string
)

# Get description of an implemented interface in Varlink IDL format
method GetInterfaceDescription(
	# Name of interface to query interface description of
	interface: string
) -> (
	# Interface description in Varlink IDL format
	description: string
)

# Error returned if a method is called on an unknown interface
error InterfaceNotFound(
	# Name of interface that was called but does not exist
	interface: string
)

# Error returned if an unknown method is called on an known interface
error MethodNotFound(
	# Name of method that was called but does not exist
	method: string
)

# Error returned if a method is called that is known but not implemented
error MethodNotImplemented(
	# Name of method that was called but is not implemented
	method: string
)

# Error returned if a method is called with an invalid parameter
error InvalidParameter(
	# Name of the invalid parameter
	parameter: string
)

# General permission error
error PermissionDenied()

# A method was called with the 'more' flag off, but it may only be called with the flag turned on
error ExpectedMore()
##### /run/systemd/resolve/io.systemd.Resolve
interface io.systemd

# Local error if a Varlink connection is disconnected (this never crosses the wire and is synthesized locally only).
error Disconnected()

# A method call time-out has been reached (also synthesized locally, does not cross wire)
error TimedOut()

# Some form of protocol error (also synthesized locally, does not cross wire)
error Protocol()

# A generic Linux system error ("errno"s).
error System(
	# The origin of this system error, typically 'linux' to indicate Linux error numbers.
	origin: ?string,
	# The Linux error name, i.e. ENOENT, EHWPOISON or similar.
	errnoName: ?string,
	# The numeric Linux error number. Typically the name is preferable, if specified.
	errno: ?int
)

# The method is implemented, but the requested operation is not supported.
error OperationNotSupported()


interface io.systemd.Resolve

# The type of protocol.
type DNSProtocol(
	# DNS
	dns,
	# Multicast DNS
	mdns,
	# LLMNR
	llmnr
)

# The mode of DNSOverTLS.
type DNSOverTLSMode(
	# DNSOverTLS is disabled.
	no,
	# DNSOverTLS is enabled.
	yes,
	# Try to use DNSOverTLS, but disabled when the server does not support it.
	opportunistic
)

# Whether the protocol is enabled.
type ResolveSupport(
	# The protocol is disabled.
	no,
	# The protocol is enabled.
	yes,
	# The protocol is used only for resolving.
	resolve
)

# The management mode of /etc/resolve.conf file.
type ResolvConfMode(
	# /etc/resolv.conf is a symbolic link to /run/systemd/resolve/resolv.conf.
	uplink,
	# /etc/resolv.conf is a symbolic link to /run/systemd/resolve/stub-resolv.conf.
	stub,
	# /etc/resolv.conf is a symbolic link to /usr/lib/systemd/resolv.conf.
	static,
	# /etc/resolv.conf does not exist.
	missing,
	# /etc/resolv.conf is not managed by systemd-resolved.
	foreign
)

# Describes the update flag for browsing services, indicating whether a service was added or removed during browsing.
type BrowseServiceUpdateFlag(
	# Indicates that the service was added.
	added,
	# Indicates that the service was removed.
	removed
)

# Encapsulates a resolved address.
type ResolvedAddress(
	ifindex: ?int,
	family: int,
	address: []int
)

# Encapsulates a resolved host name.
type ResolvedName(
	ifindex: ?int,
	name: string
)

# Encapsulates resolved service information.
type ResolvedService(
	priority: int,
	weight: int,
	port: int,
	hostname: string,
	canonicalName: ?string,
	addresses: ?[]ResolvedAddress
)

# Encapsulates the canonical name, type and domain of a DNS-SD or simple SRV service. Note that due to CNAME redirections and similar, a named DNS-SD service might resolve to a canonical service that is an unnamed simple SRV service. Or in other words: resolving a named service might return an unnamed canonical service.
type ResolvedCanonical(
	# The DNS-SD name of the service. For simple SRV services this field is absent or null.
	name: ?string,
	type: string,
	domain: string
)

# The 'key' part of a DNS resource record.
type ResourceKey(
	# The RR class, almost always IN, i.e 0x01. If unspecified defaults to IN.
	class: ?int,
	# The RR types, one of A, AAAA, PTR, …
	type: int,
	# The domain name.
	name: string
)

# A full DNS resource record..
type ResourceRecord(
	key: ResourceKey,
	priority: ?int,
	weight: ?int,
	port: ?int,
	name: ?string,
	cpu: ?string,
	os: ?string,
	items: ?[]string,
	address: ?[]int,
	mname: ?string,
	rname: ?string,
	serial: ?int,
	refresh: ?int,
	retry: ?int,
	expire: ?int,
	minimum: ?int,
	exchange: ?string,
	version: ?int,
	size: ?int,
	horiz_pre: ?int,
	vert_pre: ?int,
	latitude: ?int,
	longitude: ?int,
	altitude: ?int,
	keyTag: ?int,
	algorithm: ?int,
	digestType: ?int,
	digest: ?string,
	fptype: ?int,
	fingerprint: ?string,
	flags: ?int,
	protocol: ?int,
	dnskey: ?string,
	signer: ?string,
	typeCovered: ?int,
	labels: ?int,
	originalTtl: ?int,
	expiration: ?int,
	inception: ?int,
	signature: ?string,
	nextDomain: ?string,
	types: ?[]int,
	iterations: ?int,
	salt: ?string,
	hash: ?string,
	certUsage: ?int,
	selector: ?int,
	matchingType: ?int,
	data: ?string,
	tag: ?string,
	value: ?string,
	target: ?string,
	svcparams: ?object,
	order: ?int,
	preference: ?int,
	naptrFlags: ?string,
	services: ?string,
	regexp: ?string,
	replacement: ?string
)

# Encapsulates information about a resolved DNS resource record 
type ResolvedRecord(
	ifindex: ?int,
	rr: ?ResourceRecord,
	raw: string
)

# Encapsulates the service data obtained from browsing.
type ServiceData(
	updateFlag: BrowseServiceUpdateFlag,
	# The address family of the service, one of AF_INET or AF_INET6.
	family: int,
	# The name of the service, e.g., 'My Service'. May be null if not specified.
	name: ?string,
	# The type of service, e.g., '_http._tcp'.
	type: string,
	# The domain in which the service resides, e.g., 'local'.
	domain: string,
	# The Linux interface index for the network interface associated with this service.
	ifindex: int
)

# Encapsulates a DNS server address specification.
type DNSServer(
	# IPv4 or IPv6 address of the server.
	address: []int,
	# IPv4 or IPv6 address of the server, formatted as a human-readable string.
	addressString: ?string,
	# Address family of the server, one of AF_INET or AF_INET6.
	family: int,
	# Port number of the server.
	port: int,
	# Interface index for which this server is configured.
	ifindex: ?int,
	# Server Name Indication (SNI) of the server.
	name: ?string,
	# Indicates if the DNS server is accessible or not.
	accessible: bool
)

# Encapsulates a search domain specification.
type SearchDomain(
	# Domain name.
	name: string,
	# Indicates whether or not this is a routing-only domain.
	routeOnly: bool,
	# Interface index for which this search domain is configured.
	ifindex: ?int
)

# Encapsulates a global or per-link DNS configuration, including configured DNS servers, search domains, and more.
type DNSConfiguration(
	# Interface name, if any, associated with this configuration. Empty for global configuration.
	ifname: ?string,
	# Interface index, if any, associated with this configuration. Empty for global configuration.
	ifindex: ?int,
	# Delegate name, if any, associated with this configuration. Empty for global or link configurations.
	delegate: ?string,
	# Indicates whether or not this link's DNS servers will be used for resolving domain names that do not match any link's configured domains.
	defaultRoute: ?bool,
	# DNS server currently selected to use for lookups.
	currentServer: ?DNSServer,
	# Array of configured DNS servers.
	servers: ?[]DNSServer,
	# Array of configured fallback DNS servers, set for global configuration only.
	fallbackServers: ?[]DNSServer,
	# Array of configured search domains.
	searchDomains: ?[]SearchDomain,
	# Array of configured DNSSEC negative trust anchors.
	negativeTrustAnchors: ?[]string,
	# DNSSEC mode.
	dnssec: ?string,
	# Indicates if the current DNS server supports DNSSEC. Always false if DNSSEC mode is "no".
	dnssecSupported: ?bool,
	# DNSOverTLS mode.
	dnsOverTLS: ?DNSOverTLSMode,
	# LLMNR support.
	llmnr: ?string,
	# mDNS support.
	mDNS: ?ResolveSupport,
	# resolv.conf mode, set for global configuration only.
	resolvConfMode: ?ResolvConfMode,
	# Array of current DNS scopes.
	scopes: ?[]DNSScope
)

# Encapsulates a DNS scope specification.
type DNSScope(
	# Protocol associated with this scope.
	protocol: DNSProtocol,
	# Address family associated with this scope.
	family: ?int,
	# Interface index associated with this scope.
	ifindex: ?int,
	# Interface name associated with this scope.
	ifname: ?string,
	# DNSSEC mode associated with this scope.
	dnssec: ?string,
	# DNSOverTLS mode associated with this scope.
	dnsOverTLS: ?DNSOverTLSMode
)

# Resolves a hostname to one or more IP addresses.
method ResolveHostname(
	# The Linux interface index for the network interface to search on. Typically left unspecified, in order to search on all interfaces.
	ifindex: ?int,
	# The host name to resolve.
	name: string,
	# The address family to search for, one of AF_INET or AF_INET6.
	family: ?int,
	# Various search flags.
	flags: ?int
) -> (
	# Array of resolved IP addresses
	addresses: []ResolvedAddress,
	# Canonical name of the host.
	name: string,
	# Various flags indicating details on discovered data.
	flags: int
)

# Resolves an IP address to a hostname.
method ResolveAddress(
	# The Linux interface index for the network interface to search on. Typically left unspecified, in order to search on all interfaces.
	ifindex: ?int,
	# The address family of the specified address, one of AF_INET or AF_INET6.
	family: int,
	# The IP address to look up, either 4 or 16 integers (depending if an AF_INET or AF_INET6 address shall be resolved).
	address: []int,
	flags: ?int
) -> (
	names: []ResolvedName,
	flags: int
)

# Resolves a named DNS-SD or unnamed simple SRV service.
method ResolveService(
	name: ?string,
	type: ?string,
	domain: string,
	ifindex: ?int,
	family: ?int,
	flags: ?int
) -> (
	services: []ResolvedService,
	txt: ?[]string,
	canonical: ResolvedCanonical,
	flags: int
)

# Resolves a domain name to one or more DNS resource records.
method ResolveRecord(
	ifindex: ?int,
	name: string,
	class: ?int,
	type: int,
	flags: ?int
) -> (
	rrs: []ResolvedRecord,
	flags: int
)

# Starts browsing for DNS-SD services of specified type.
# [Requires 'more' flag]
method BrowseServices(
	# The domain to browse for services. If null, the default browsing domain local is used.
	domain: ?string,
	# The service type to browse for (e.g., '_http._tcp').
	type: ?string,
	# The Linux interface index for the network interface to search on.
	ifindex: ?int,
	# Various browsing flags to modify the operation.
	flags: ?int
) -> (
	# An array of service data containing information about discovered services.
	browserServiceData: []ServiceData
)

# Current global and per-link DNS configurations.
method DumpDNSConfiguration() -> (
	# The current global and per-interface DNS configurations
	configuration: []DNSConfiguration
)

error NoNameServers()

error NoSuchResourceRecord()

error QueryTimedOut()

error MaxAttemptsReached()

error InvalidReply()

error QueryAborted()

error QueryRefused()

error DNSSECValidationFailed(
	result: string,
	extendedDNSErrorCode: ?int,
	extendedDNSErrorMessage: ?string
)

error NoTrustAnchor()

error ResourceRecordTypeUnsupported()

error NetworkDown()

error NoSource()

error StubLoop()

error DNSError(
	rcode: int,
	queryString: string,
	extendedDNSErrorCode: ?int,
	extendedDNSErrorMessage: ?string
)

error CNAMELoop()

error BadAddressSize()

error ResourceRecordTypeInvalidForQuery()

error ZoneTransfersNotPermitted()

error ResourceRecordTypeObsolete()

# The DNS resource records of the specified service are not consistent (e.g. lacks a DNS-SD service type when resolved).
error InconsistentServiceRecords()

# The service is explicitly not provided on the queried domain (RFC 2782 root domain SRV record).
error ServiceNotProvided()


interface io.systemd.Resolve.Link

# DNS server configuration input.
type DNSServerContext(
	# IPv4 or IPv6 address of the server.
	address: []int,
	# Address family of the server, one of AF_INET or AF_INET6.
	family: int,
	# Port number of the server.
	port: ?int,
	# Interface index for which this server is configured.
	ifindex: ?int,
	# Server Name Indication (SNI) of the server.
	name: ?string
)

# Set the per-link DNS server list.
method SetDNS(
	# Controls whether interactive authentication (via polkit) shall be allowed. If unspecified defaults to false.
	allowInteractiveAuthentication: ?bool,
	# Index of the interface to configure.
	ifindex: int,
	# DNS servers to configure on the link
	servers: []DNSServerContext
) -> ()

# The specified interface is managed by systemd-networkd. The request should be re-directed to systemd-networkd.
error ManagedByNetworkd()

# The specified interface is a loopback interface, and cannot be configured.
error IsLoopback()

	# Controls whether interactive authentication (via polkit) shall be allowed. If unspecified defaults to false.
	allowInteractiveAuthentication: ?bool
) -> ()

) -> ()

	# The current maximum log level, using BSD syslog level integers.
	level: int
)

	# Returns the current environment block, i.e. the contents of environ[].
	environment: ?[]string
)

# Returned if the environment block is currently not in a valid state.
error InconsistentEnvironment()

)

	insecure: int,
	bogus: int,
	indeterminate: int
)

	PacketRRSIGMissing: bool,
	PacketInvalid: bool,
	PacketDoOff: bool
)

	# IPv4 or IPv6 address of the server.
	address: []int,

# Encapsulates a global or per-link DNS configuration, including configured DNS servers, search domains, and more.
type DNSConfiguration(
	# Interface name, if any, associated with this configuration. Empty for global configuration.
	ifname: ?string,
	# Interface index, if any, associated with this configuration. Empty for global configuration.
	ifindex: ?int,
	# Delegate name, if any, associated with this configuration. Empty for global or link configurations.
	delegate: ?string,
	# Indicates whether or not this link's DNS servers will be used for resolving domain names that do not match any link's configured domains.
	defaultRoute: ?bool,
	# DNS server currently selected to use for lookups.
	currentServer: ?DNSServer,
	# Array of configured DNS servers.
	servers: ?[]DNSServer,
	# Array of configured fallback DNS servers, set for global configuration only.
	fallbackServers: ?[]DNSServer,
	# Array of configured search domains.
	searchDomains: ?[]SearchDomain,
	# Array of configured DNSSEC negative trust anchors.
	negativeTrustAnchors: ?[]string,
	# DNSSEC mode.
	dnssec: ?string,
	# Indicates if the current DNS server supports DNSSEC. Always false if DNSSEC mode is "no".
	dnssecSupported: ?bool,
	# DNSOverTLS mode.
	dnsOverTLS: ?DNSOverTLSMode,
	# LLMNR support.
	llmnr: ?string,
	# mDNS support.
	mDNS: ?ResolveSupport,
	# resolv.conf mode, set for global configuration only.
	resolvConfMode: ?ResolvConfMode,
	# Array of current DNS scopes.
	scopes: ?[]DNSScope
)

	# Address family associated with this scope.
	family: ?int,
	# Interface index associated with this scope.
	ifindex: ?int,
	# Interface name associated with this scope.
	ifname: ?string,
	# DNSSEC mode associated with this scope.
	dnssec: ?string,
	# DNSOverTLS mode associated with this scope.
	dnsOverTLS: ?DNSOverTLSMode
)

# [Requires 'more' flag]

	# Controls whether interactive authentication (via polkit) shall be allowed. If unspecified defaults to false.
	allowInteractiveAuthentication: ?bool
) -> (
	ready: ?bool,
	state: ?string,
	result: ?string,
	rcode: ?int,
	errno: ?int,

	# Controls whether interactive authentication (via polkit) shall be allowed. If unspecified defaults to false.
	allowInteractiveAuthentication: ?bool
) -> ()

	allowInteractiveAuthentication: ?bool
) -> (
	# The current global and per-interface DNS configurations
	configuration: []DNSConfiguration
)

# Flushes all DNS resource record caches the service maintains locally.
method FlushCaches(
	# Controls whether interactive authentication (via polkit) shall be allowed. If unspecified defaults to false.
	allowInteractiveAuthentication: ?bool
) -> ()

# Flushes all feature level information the resolver learnt about specific servers, and ensures that the server feature probing logic is started from the beginning with the next look-up request.
method ResetServerFeatures(
	# Controls whether interactive authentication (via polkit) shall be allowed. If unspecified defaults to false.
	allowInteractiveAuthentication: ?bool
) -> ()


# General Varlink service interface
interface org.varlink.service

# Get service meta information
method GetInfo() -> (
	# String identifying the vendor of this service
	vendor: string,
	# String identifying the product implementing this service
	product: string,
	# Version string of this product
	version: string,
	# Web URL pointing to additional information about this service
	url: string,
	# List of interfaces implemented by this service
	interfaces: []string
)

# Get description of an implemented interface in Varlink IDL format
method GetInterfaceDescription(
	# Name of interface to query interface description of
	interface: string
) -> (
	# Interface description in Varlink IDL format
	description: string
)

# Error returned if a method is called on an unknown interface
error InterfaceNotFound(
	# Name of interface that was called but does not exist
	interface: string
)

# Error returned if an unknown method is called on an known interface
error MethodNotFound(
	# Name of method that was called but does not exist
	method: string
)

# Error returned if a method is called that is known but not implemented
error MethodNotImplemented(
	# Name of method that was called but is not implemented
	method: string
)

# Error returned if a method is called with an invalid parameter
error InvalidParameter(
	# Name of the invalid parameter
	parameter: string
)

# General permission error
error PermissionDenied()

# A method was called with the 'more' flag off, but it may only be called with the flag turned on
error ExpectedMore()
##### /run/systemd/netif/io.systemd.Network
interface io.systemd

# Local error if a Varlink connection is disconnected (this never crosses the wire and is synthesized locally only).
error Disconnected()

# A method call time-out has been reached (also synthesized locally, does not cross wire)
error TimedOut()

# Some form of protocol error (also synthesized locally, does not cross wire)
error Protocol()

# A generic Linux system error ("errno"s).
error System(
	# The origin of this system error, typically 'linux' to indicate Linux error numbers.
	origin: ?string,
	# The Linux error name, i.e. ENOENT, EHWPOISON or similar.
	errnoName: ?string,
	# The numeric Linux error number. Typically the name is preferable, if specified.
	errno: ?int
)

# The method is implemented, but the requested operation is not supported.
error OperationNotSupported()


interface io.systemd.Network

type Address(
	# Address family (AF_INET or AF_INET6)
	Family: int,
	# IP address
	Address: []int,
	# IP address (human-readable format)
	AddressString: string,
	# Peer address for point-to-point interfaces
	Peer: ?[]int,
	# Peer address for point-to-point interfaces (human-readable format)
	PeerString: ?string,
	# Network prefix length
	PrefixLength: int,
	# Configuration source for this address
	ConfigSource: string,
	# Address of the configuration provider (DHCP server, router, etc.)
	ConfigProvider: ?[]int,
	# Address of the configuration provider (DHCP server, router, etc.) (human-readable format)
	ConfigProviderString: ?string,
	# Broadcast address for IPv4 networks
	Broadcast: ?[]int,
	# Broadcast address for IPv4 networks (human-readable format)
	BroadcastString: ?string,
	# Address scope (RT_SCOPE_* value)
	Scope: ?int,
	# Human-readable scope string
	ScopeString: ?string,
	# Address flags (IFA_F_* values)
	Flags: ?int,
	# Human-readable flags string
	FlagsString: ?string,
	# Address label for IPv4
	Label: ?string,
	# Preferred lifetime in microseconds
	PreferredLifetimeUSec: ?int,
	# Preferred lifetime in microseconds (deprecated, for backward compatibility)
	PreferredLifetimeUsec: ?int,
	# Valid lifetime in microseconds
	ValidLifetimeUSec: ?int,
	# Valid lifetime in microseconds (deprecated, for backward compatibility)
	ValidLifetimeUsec: ?int,
	# Configuration state of this address
	ConfigState: ?string
)

type BitRates(
	# Transmit bitrate in bits per second
	TxBitRate: int,
	# Receive bitrate in bits per second
	RxBitRate: int
)

type DHCPLease(
	# Timestamp when the lease was acquired in microseconds
	LeaseTimestampUSec: ?int,
	# T1 timeout (lease renewal time) in microseconds
	Timeout1USec: ?int,
	# T2 timeout (lease rebinding time) in microseconds
	Timeout2USec: ?int,
	# Hostname received from DHCP server
	Hostname: ?string
)

type DHCPServer(
	# Offset from the network address for the DHCP address pool
	PoolOffset: int,
	# Size of the DHCP address pool
	PoolSize: int,
	# Active DHCP leases assigned by the server
	Leases: ?[]DHCPServerLease,
	# Static DHCP leases configured for specific clients
	StaticLeases: ?[]DHCPServerLease
)

type DHCPServerLease(
	# DHCP client identifier
	ClientId: ?[]int,
	# Address assigned to the client
	Address: ?[]int,
	# Address assigned to the client (human-readable format)
	AddressString: ?string,
	# Hostname provided by the DHCP client
	Hostname: ?string,
	# Hardware address type (ARPHRD_* value)
	HardwareAddressType: ?int,
	# Length of hardware address in bytes
	HardwareAddressLength: ?int,
	# Hardware (MAC) address of the DHCP client
	HardwareAddress: ?[]int,
	# Lease expiration time in monotonic microseconds
	ExpirationUSec: ?int,
	# Lease expiration time in realtime microseconds
	ExpirationRealtimeUSec: ?int
)

type DHCPv6Client(
	# State of the DHCPv6 client
	State: ?DHCPv6ClientState,
	# DHCPv6 lease information including timestamps and timeouts
	Lease: ?DHCPLease,
	# Delegated prefixes received from DHCPv6 server
	Prefixes: ?[]DHCPv6ClientPD,
	# Vendor-specific options received in DHCPv6 response
	VendorSpecificOptions: ?[]DHCPv6ClientVendorOption,
	# DHCP Unique Identifier (DUID) of the client
	DUID: []int
)

	Prefix: ?[]int,
	# Human-readable delegated IPv6 prefix
	PrefixString: ?string,
	# Length of the delegated prefix in bits
	PrefixLength: int,
	# Preferred lifetime of the prefix in microseconds
	PreferredLifetimeUSec: ?int,
	# Valid lifetime of the prefix in microseconds
	ValidLifetimeUSec: ?int
)

	# Vendor-specific sub-option code
	SubOptionCode: int,
	# Raw data of the vendor-specific sub-option
	SubOptionData: string
)

type DNS(
	# Address family (AF_INET or AF_INET6)
	Family: int,
	# DNS server IP address
	Address: []int,
	# DNS server IP address (human-readable format)
	AddressString: string,
	# DNS server port number
	Port: ?int,
	# Interface index for link-local DNS servers
	InterfaceIndex: ?int,

)

)

)

type Domain(
	# DNS search or route domain name
	Domain: string,
	# Configuration source for this domain
	ConfigSource: string,
	# Address of the configuration provider
	ConfigProvider: ?[]int,
	# Address of the configuration provider (human-readable format)
	ConfigProviderString: ?string
)

type Interface(
	# Network interface index
	Index: int,
	# Primary interface name
	Name: string,
	# Alternative interface names
	AlternativeNames: ?[]string,
	# Interface index of the master device (for bridge/bond slaves)
	MasterInterfaceIndex: ?int,
	# Interface kind (vlan, bridge, bond, etc.)
	Kind: ?string,
	# Interface type (ether, loopback, etc.)
	Type: string,
	# Kernel driver name
	Driver: ?string,
	# Interface flags (IFF_* values)
	Flags: int,
	# Human-readable interface flags
	FlagsString: string,
	# Kernel operational state (IF_OPER_* value)
	KernelOperationalState: int,
	# Human-readable kernel operational state
	KernelOperationalStateString: string,
	# Current MTU in bytes
	MTU: int,
	# Minimum allowed MTU in bytes
	MinimumMTU: int,
	# Maximum allowed MTU in bytes
	MaximumMTU: int,
	# Current hardware (MAC) address
	HardwareAddress: ?[]int,
	# Permanent hardware (MAC) address from factory
	PermanentHardwareAddress: ?[]int,
	# Broadcast address for Ethernet interfaces
	BroadcastAddress: ?[]int,
	# IPv6 link-local address
	IPv6LinkLocalAddress: ?[]int,
	# IPv6 link-local address (human-readable format)
	IPv6LinkLocalAddressString: ?string,
	# Wireless LAN interface type (NL80211_IFTYPE_* value)
	WirelessLanInterfaceType: ?int,
	# Human-readable wireless LAN interface type
	WirelessLanInterfaceTypeString: ?string,
	# Wireless network SSID
	SSID: ?string,
	# Wireless access point BSSID (MAC address)
	BSSID: ?[]int,
	# Administrative state (configuring, configured, etc.)
	AdministrativeState: LinkState,
	# Why the link is still in the configuring state, set only in that state
	ConfiguringReasons: ?[]LinkConfiguringReason,
	# Operational state (routable, degraded, etc.)
	OperationalState: string,
	# Carrier state (carrier, no-carrier, etc.)
	CarrierState: string,
	# Address configuration state
	AddressState: LinkAddressState,
	# IPv4 address configuration state
	IPv4AddressState: LinkAddressState,
	# IPv6 address configuration state
	IPv6AddressState: LinkAddressState,
	# Online state (online, offline, etc.)
	OnlineState: ?LinkOnlineState,
	# Path to .network configuration file
	NetworkFile: ?string,
	# Paths to .network drop-in configuration files
	NetworkFileDropins: ?[]string,
	# Whether this interface is required for online state
	RequiredForOnline: ?bool,
	# Required operational state range for considering interface online [min, max]
	RequiredOperationalStateForOnline: ?[]string,
	# Required address family for considering interface online
	RequiredFamilyForOnline: ?LinkRequiredAddressFamily,
	# Activation policy (up, always-up, manual, etc.)
	ActivationPolicy: ?string,
	# Path to .netdev configuration file
	NetDevFile: ?string,
	# Paths to .netdev drop-in configuration files
	NetDevFileDropins: ?[]string,
	# Path to .link configuration file
	LinkFile: ?string,
	# Paths to .link drop-in configuration files
	LinkFileDropins: ?[]string,
	# Device path in sysfs
	Path: ?string,
	# Hardware vendor name
	Vendor: ?string,
	# Hardware model name
	Model: ?string,
	# DNS servers configured for this interface
	DNS: ?[]DNS,
	# Discovery of Network-designated Resolvers (RFC9463)
	DNR: ?[]object,
	# NTP servers configured for this interface
	NTP: ?[]NTP,
	# SIP servers configured for this interface
	SIP: ?[]SIP,
	# DNS search domains
	SearchDomains: ?[]Domain,
	# DNS route-only domains
	RouteDomains: ?[]Domain,
	# DNSSEC negative trust anchors
	DNSSECNegativeTrustAnchors: ?[]DNSSECNegativeTrustAnchor,
	# DNS resolution settings (LLMNR, mDNS, etc.)
	DNSSettings: ?[]DNSSetting,
	# Captive portal detection URL (RFC8910)
	CaptivePortal: ?string,
	# IPv6 Neighbor Discovery information
	NDisc: ?NDisc,
	# IP addresses configured on this interface
	Addresses: ?[]Address,
	# Neighbor entries (ARP/NDP cache)
	Neighbors: ?[]Neighbor,
	# Next hops for multipath routing
	NextHops: ?[]NextHop,
	# Routes configured on this interface
	Routes: ?[]Route,
	# DHCP server configuration and leases
	DHCPServer: ?DHCPServer,
	# DHCPv4 client configuration and lease information
	DHCPv4Client: ?object,
	# DHCPv6 client configuration and lease information
	DHCPv6Client: ?DHCPv6Client,
	# LLDP transmit configuration for this interface
	LLDP: ?LLDPNeighbor,
	# Current transmit/receive bitrates from speed meter
	BitRates: ?BitRates
)

type LLDPNeighbor(
	# Chassis identifier in human-readable format
	ChassisID: ?string,
	# Raw chassis identifier bytes
	RawChassisID: []int,
	# Port identifier in human-readable format
	PortID: string,
	# Raw port identifier bytes
	RawPortID: []int,
	# Description of the port
	PortDescription: ?string,
	# System name of the LLDP neighbor
	SystemName: ?string,
	# System description of the LLDP neighbor
	SystemDescription: ?string,
	# Enabled capabilities bitmap (bridge, router, WLAN, etc.)
	EnabledCapabilities: int,
	# Manufacturer Usage Description URL (RFC8520)
	MUDURL: ?string,
	# VLAN identifier
	VlanID: ?int
)

type LLDPNeighborsByInterface(
	# Network interface index
	InterfaceIndex: int,
	# Primary interface name
	InterfaceName: string,
	# Alternative interface names
	InterfaceAlternativeNames: ?[]string,
	# LLDP neighbors discovered on this interface
	Neighbors: []LLDPNeighbor
)

type NDisc(
	# PREF64 (RFC8781) prefixes advertised via IPv6 Router Advertisements
	PREF64: ?[]Pref64
)

type Neighbor(
	# Address family (AF_INET or AF_INET6)
	Family: int,
	# IP address of the neighbor
	Destination: []int,
	# IP address of the neighbor (human-readable format)
	DestinationString: string,
	# Link layer (MAC) address of the neighbor
	LinkLayerAddress: []int,
	# Configuration source for this neighbor entry
	ConfigSource: string,
	# Configuration state of this neighbor entry
	ConfigState: string
)

type NextHop(
	# Next hop identifier
	ID: int,
	# Address family (AF_INET or AF_INET6)
	Family: int,
	# Gateway address for this next hop
	Gateway: ?[]int,
	# Gateway address for this next hop (human-readable format)
	GatewayString: ?string,
	# Next hop flags (RTNH_F_* values)
	Flags: ?int,
	# Human-readable flags string
	FlagsString: ?string,
	# Routing protocol identifier
	Protocol: ?int,
	# Human-readable routing protocol name
	ProtocolString: ?string,
	# Whether this is a blackhole next hop (drops packets)
	Blackhole: ?bool,
	# Multipath group members with weights for load balancing
	Group: ?[]NextHopGroup,
	# Configuration source for this next hop
	ConfigSource: string,
	# Address of the configuration provider
	ConfigProvider: ?[]int,
	# Address of the configuration provider (human-readable format)
	ConfigProviderString: ?string,
	# Configuration state of this next hop
	ConfigState: ?string
)

type NextHopGroup(
	# Next hop identifier in the multipath group
	ID: int,
	# Weight for load balancing (higher means more traffic)
	Weight: int
)

type NTP(
	# Address family (AF_INET or AF_INET6) for address-based servers
	Family: ?int,
	# NTP server IP address
	Address: ?[]int,
	# NTP server IP address (human-readable format)
	AddressString: ?string,
	# NTP server
	Server: ?string,
	# Configuration source for this NTP server
	ConfigSource: string,
	# Address of the configuration provider
	ConfigProvider: ?[]int,
	# Address of the configuration provider (human-readable format)
	ConfigProviderString: ?string
)

type Pref64(
	# IPv6 prefix for NAT64/DNS64
	Prefix: ?[]int,
	# Human-readable IPv6 prefix
	PrefixString: ?string,
	# Length of the prefix in bits
	PrefixLength: int,
	# Lifetime of the prefix in microseconds
	LifetimeUSec: ?int,
	# Address of router that provided this prefix
	ConfigProvider: ?[]int,
	# Address of router that provided this prefix (human-readable format)
	ConfigProviderString: ?string
)

type PrivateOption(
	# DHCP option number
	Option: ?int,
	# Raw data of the private DHCP option
	PrivateOptionData: ?string
)

type Route(
	# Address family (AF_INET or AF_INET6)
	Family: int,
	# Destination network address
	Destination: []int,
	# Destination network address (human-readable format)
	DestinationString: string,
	# Destination network prefix length
	DestinationPrefixLength: int,
	# Gateway address for this route
	Gateway: ?[]int,
	# Gateway address for this route (human-readable format)
	GatewayString: ?string,
	# Source address prefix for route selection
	Source: ?[]int,
	# Source address prefix for route selection (human-readable format)
	SourceString: ?string,
	# Source prefix length
	SourcePrefixLength: ?int,
	# Preferred source address for outgoing packets
	PreferredSource: ?[]int,
	# Preferred source address for outgoing packets (human-readable format)
	PreferredSourceString: ?string,
	# Type of Service (TOS) field
	TOS: int,
	# Route scope (RT_SCOPE_* value)
	Scope: int,
	# Human-readable scope string
	ScopeString: ?string,
	# Routing protocol identifier
	Protocol: int,
	# Human-readable routing protocol name
	ProtocolString: ?string,
	# Route type (RTN_* value)
	Type: int,
	# Human-readable route type
	TypeString: ?string,
	# Route priority/metric
	Priority: int,
	# Routing table number
	Table: int,
	# Human-readable routing table name
	TableString: ?string,
	# Route flags (RTM_F_* values)
	Flags: int,
	# Human-readable flags string
	FlagsString: ?string,
	# Next hop ID for multipath routing
	NextHopID: ?int,
	# Maximum Transmission Unit for this route
	MTU: ?int,
	# Route preference for IPv6 (low/medium/high)
	Preference: ?int,
	# Route lifetime in microseconds
	LifetimeUSec: ?int,
	# Configuration source for this route
	ConfigSource: string,
	# Address of the configuration provider
	ConfigProvider: ?[]int,
	# Address of the configuration provider (human-readable format)
	ConfigProviderString: ?string,
	# Configuration state of this route
	ConfigState: ?string,
	# Interface index for serialization
	InterfaceIndex: ?int,
	# Route metrics (RTAX_* values)
	Metrics: ?[]int,
	# TCP congestion control algorithm for this route
	TCPCongestionControlAlgorithm: ?string
)

type RoutingPolicyRule(
	# Address family (AF_INET or AF_INET6)
	Family: int,
	# Source address prefix to match
	FromPrefix: ?[]int,
	# Source address prefix to match (human-readable format)
	FromPrefixString: ?string,
	# Length of source prefix in bits
	FromPrefixLength: ?int,
	# Destination address prefix to match
	ToPrefix: ?[]int,
	# Destination address prefix to match (human-readable format)
	ToPrefixString: ?string,
	# Length of destination prefix in bits
	ToPrefixLength: ?int,
	# Routing protocol identifier
	Protocol: int,
	# Human-readable routing protocol name
	ProtocolString: string,
	# Type of Service (TOS) field to match
	TOS: int,
	# Rule action type (FR_ACT_* value)
	Type: int,
	# Human-readable rule action type
	TypeString: string,
	# IP protocol number to match
	IPProtocol: int,
	# Human-readable IP protocol name
	IPProtocolString: string,
	# Rule priority (lower value means higher priority)
	Priority: int,
	# Firewall mark value to match
	FirewallMark: int,
	# Firewall mark mask to apply
	FirewallMask: int,
	# Routing table number to use
	Table: ?int,
	# Human-readable routing table name
	TableString: ?string,
	# Whether rule matching is inverted
	Invert: bool,
	# Suppress route lookup if prefix length is less than or equal to this value
	SuppressPrefixLength: ?int,
	# Suppress route lookup if interface group matches
	SuppressInterfaceGroup: ?int,
	# Source port range to match [start, end]
	SourcePort: ?[]int,
	# Destination port range to match [start, end]
	DestinationPort: ?[]int,
	# User ID range to match [start, end]
	User: ?[]int,
	# Incoming interface name to match
	IncomingInterface: ?string,
	# Outgoing interface name to match
	OutgoingInterface: ?string,
	# Configuration source for this rule
	ConfigSource: string,
	# Configuration state of this rule
	ConfigState: string
)

type SIP(
	# Address family (AF_INET or AF_INET6) for address-based servers
	Family: ?int,
	# SIP server IP address
	Address: ?[]int,
	# SIP server IP address (human-readable format)
	AddressString: ?string,
	# SIP server domain name
	Domain: ?string,
	# Configuration source for this SIP server
	ConfigSource: string,
	# Address of the configuration provider
	ConfigProvider: ?[]int,
	# Address of the configuration provider (human-readable format)
	ConfigProviderString: ?string
)

method Describe() -> (
	# All network interfaces managed by systemd-networkd
	Interfaces: ?[]Interface,
	# All configured next hops for multipath routing
	NextHops: ?[]NextHop,
	# All configured routes across all interfaces
	Routes: ?[]Route,
	# All configured routing policy rules
	RoutingPolicyRules: ?[]RoutingPolicyRule
)

method GetStates() -> (
	# Overall address configuration state across all interfaces
	AddressState: LinkAddressState,
	# IPv4 address configuration state across all interfaces
	IPv4AddressState: LinkAddressState,
	# IPv6 address configuration state across all interfaces
	IPv6AddressState: LinkAddressState,
	# Carrier state across all interfaces
	CarrierState: string,
	# Online state across all interfaces
	OnlineState: ?LinkOnlineState,
	# Operational state across all interfaces
	OperationalState: string
)

method GetNamespaceId() -> (
	# Network namespace inode number
	NamespaceId: int,
	# Network namespace ID (cookie) assigned by the kernel
	NamespaceNSID: ?int
)

method GetLLDPNeighbors(
	# Filter by interface index (optional)
	InterfaceIndex: ?int,
	# Filter by interface name (optional)
	InterfaceName: ?string
) -> (
	# LLDP neighbors grouped by interface
	Neighbors: []LLDPNeighborsByInterface
)

method Reload(
	# If true (default), reload config files and reconfigure all matching network interfaces. If false, only reload config files without reconfiguring interfaces; use 'networkctl reconfigure' afterwards to apply changes selectively.
	reconfigureLinks: ?bool,
	# Controls whether interactive authentication (via polkit) shall be allowed. If unspecified defaults to false.
	allowInteractiveAuthentication: ?bool
) -> ()

method SetPersistentStorage(
	# Whether persistent storage is ready and writable
	Ready: bool
) -> ()

error AlreadyReloading()

error StorageReadOnly()


interface io.systemd.Network.Link

	stopped,
	information_request,
	solicitation,
	request,
	bound,
	renew,
	rebind,
	stopping
)
