# Storage Provider API, a generic interface for acquiring access to storage volumes
interface io.systemd.StorageProvider

# Encodes three classes of storage volumes. This follows the kernel's nomenclature for inode types, i.e. reg, dir, blk.
type VolumeType(
	# Block device storage volumes, block-addressable
	blk,
	# Regular file storage volumes, byte-addressable
	reg,
	# POSIX file system storage volumes, path/offset-addressable
	dir
)

# Determines whether to open existing or create a new storage volume.
type CreateMode(
	# Open if exists already, create if missing
	any,
	# Create if missing, fail if exists already
	new,
	# Open if exists already, fail if missing
	open
)

# Acquires a file descriptor for a storage volume.
method Acquire(
	# The name of the storage volume to acquire
	name: string,
	# Determines whether to open or create a storage volume
	createMode: ?CreateMode,
	# The template to use when creating a new storage volume
	template: ?string,
	# Controls read/write access to the storage volume. If false and the storage volume cannot be opened in writable mode the call will fail. If null, storage volume will be acquired in writable mode if possible, read-only otherwise. If true, storage volume will be opened in read-only mode (and fail if that's not possible).
	readOnly: ?bool,
	# Dictates what kind of storage volume to request. Some storage volumes can be acquired either as regular file or as block device. In all other cases if this value doesn't match the volume type, the request will fail.
	requestAs: ?VolumeType,
	# The size of the storage volume, if one is created. Has no effect if no storage volume is created.
	createSizeBytes: ?int,
	# Controls whether interactive authentication (via polkit) shall be allowed. If unspecified defaults to false.
	allowInteractiveAuthentication: ?bool
) -> (
	# Returns an index into the array of file descriptors associated with this reply. This may be used to get the file descriptor of the volume. The file descriptor must be properly opened, i.e. not an O_PATH file descriptor.
	fileDescriptorIndex: int,
	# The storage volume type, i.e. ultimately the inode type of the returned file descriptor
	type: VolumeType,
	# Whether storage volume has been opened in read-only mode
	readOnly: bool,
	# Base UID for the returned file descriptor (if directory). If not specified shall default to 0.
	baseUID: ?int,
	# Base GID for the returned file descriptor (if directory). If not specified shall default to 0.
	baseGID: ?int
)

# Lists available storage volumes.
# [Requires 'more' flag]
method ListVolumes(
	# Specifies a shell glob to filter enumeration by
	matchName: ?string
) -> (
	# The storage volume's primary name
	name: string,
	# Additional names
	aliases: ?[]string,
	# The type of the storage volume
	type: VolumeType,
	# Whether the storage volume is read-only.
	readOnly: ?bool,
	# Size in bytes, if known
	sizeBytes: ?int,
	# Used bytes, if known
	usedBytes: ?int
)

# Lists available templates.
# [Requires 'more' flag]
method ListTemplates(
	# Specifies a shell glob to filter enumeration by
	matchName: ?string
) -> (
	# The template's name
	name: string,
	# The type of the storage volumes defined by this template
	type: VolumeType
)

# No storage volume under the specified name exists.
error NoSuchVolume()

# A storage volume under the specified name already exists.
error VolumeExists()

# No template under the specified name exists.
error NoSuchTemplate()

# The specified volume type is not supported by this backend or system.
error TypeNotSupported()

# The volume's type does not match the requested volume type.
error WrongType()

# This backend does not support storage volume creation of the requested type.
error CreateNotSupported()

# This backend or selected volume type requires a storage volume size to be specified if the storage volume does not exist yet and needs to be created.
error CreateSizeRequired()

# A storage volume was to be acquired in writable mode, but only read-only access is permitted.
error ReadOnlyVolume()

# Template not suitable for this storage volume type.
error BadTemplate()

	# The Linux error name, i.e. ENOENT, EHWPOISON or similar.
	errnoName: ?string,
	# The numeric Linux error number. Typically the name is preferable, if specified.
	errno: ?int
)

# The method is implemented, but the requested operation is not supported.
error OperationNotSupported()


# Journal control APIs
interface io.systemd.Journal

# Write out all pending log messages out to disk, and reply only after that's complete.
method Synchronize(
	# Controls whether to offline the journal files as part of the synchronization operation.
	offline: ?bool
) -> ()

# Rotate journal files, i.e. close existing files, start new ones.
method Rotate() -> ()

# Flush runtime logs to persistent logs, i.e. flush log data from /run/ into /var/, and continue writing future log data to the latter location.
method FlushToVar() -> ()

# Relinquish use of /var/ again, return to do runtime logging into /run/ only.
method RelinquishVar() -> ()

# Journal service running as per-namespace instance, and requested operation is not supported for namespaced journal.
error NotSupportedByNamespaces()


# An interface to control basic properties of systemd services.
interface io.systemd.service

# Checks if the service is running.
method Ping() -> ()

# Reloads configuration files.
method Reload(
	# Controls whether interactive authentication (via polkit) shall be allowed. If unspecified defaults to false.
	allowInteractiveAuthentication: ?bool
) -> ()

# Sets the maximum log level.
method SetLogLevel(
	# The maximum log level, using BSD syslog log level integers.
	level: ?int
) -> ()

# Gets the maximum log level.
method GetLogLevel() -> (
	# The current maximum log level, using BSD syslog level integers.
	level: int
)

# Get current environment block.
method GetEnvironment() -> (
	# Returns the current environment block, i.e. the contents of environ[].
	environment: ?[]string
)

# Returned if the environment block is currently not in a valid state.
error InconsistentEnvironment()


# General Varlink service interface
interface org.varlink.service

# Get service meta information
method GetInfo() -> (
	# String identifying the vendor of this service
	vendor: string,
	# String identifying the product implementing this service
	product: string,
	# Version string of this product
	version: string,
	# Web URL pointing to additional information about this service
	url: string,
	# List of interfaces implemented by this service
	interfaces: []string
)

# Get description of an implemented interface in Varlink IDL format
method GetInterfaceDescription(
	# Name of interface to query interface description of
	interface: string
) -> (
	# Interface description in Varlink IDL format
	description: string
)

# Error returned if a method is called on an unknown interface
error InterfaceNotFound(
	# Name of interface that was called but does not exist
	interface: string
)

# Error returned if an unknown method is called on an known interface
error MethodNotFound(
	# Name of method that was called but does not exist
	method: string
)

# Error returned if a method is called that is known but not implemented
error MethodNotImplemented(
	# Name of method that was called but is not implemented
	method: string
)

# Error returned if a method is called with an invalid parameter
error InvalidParameter(
	# Name of the invalid parameter
	parameter: string
)

# General permission error
error PermissionDenied()

# A method was called with the 'more' flag off, but it may only be called with the flag turned on
error ExpectedMore()

##### /run/systemd/userdb/io.systemd.DropIn
interface io.systemd

# Local error if a Varlink connection is disconnected (this never crosses the wire and is synthesized locally only).
error Disconnected()

# A method call time-out has been reached (also synthesized locally, does not cross wire)
error TimedOut()

# Some form of protocol error (also synthesized locally, does not cross wire)
error Protocol()

# A generic Linux system error ("errno"s).
error System(
	# The origin of this system error, typically 'linux' to indicate Linux error numbers.
	origin: ?string,
	# The Linux error name, i.e. ENOENT, EHWPOISON or similar.
	errnoName: ?string,
	# The numeric Linux error number. Typically the name is preferable, if specified.
	errno: ?int
)

# The method is implemented, but the requested operation is not supported.
error OperationNotSupported()


# APIs for querying user and group records.
interface io.systemd.UserDatabase

# Retrieve one or more user records. Look-up is either keyed by UID or user name, or if neither is specified all known records are enumerated.
# [Supports 'more' flag]
method GetUserRecord(
	# The numeric 32bit UNIX UID of the record, if look-up by UID is desired.
	uid: ?int,
	# The UNIX user name of the record, if look-up by name is desired.
	userName: ?string,
	# The userdb provider service to search on. Must be set to the base name of the userdb entrypoint socket. This is necessary in order to support services that implement multiple userdb services on the same socket.
	# Names to search for in a fuzzy fashion.
	fuzzyNames: ?[]string,
	# User dispositions to limit search by.
	dispositionMask: ?[]string,
	# Minimum UID to restrict search too.
	uidMin: ?int,
	# Maximum UID to restrict search too.
	uidMax: ?int,
	# The UUID to restrict search to.
	uuid: ?string,
	# The userdb provider to search on. Must be set to the name of the userdb entrypoint socket. This is necessary in order to support services that implement multiple userdb services on the same socket.
	service: string
) -> (
	# The retrieved user record.
	record: object,
	# If set to true, indicates that the user record is not complete, i.e. that the 'privileged' section has been stripped because the client lacks the privileges to access it.
	incomplete: ?bool
)

# Retrieve one or more group records. Look-up is either keyed by GID or group name, or if neither is specified all known records are enumerated.
# [Supports 'more' flag]
method GetGroupRecord(
	# The numeric 32bit UNIX GID of the record, if look-up by GID is desired.
	gid: ?int,
	# The UNIX group name of the record, if look-up by name is desired.
	groupName: ?string,
	# The userdb provider service to search on. Must be set to the base name of the userdb entrypoint socket. This is necessary in order to support services that implement multiple userdb services on the same socket.
	# Additional names to search for in a fuzzy fashion.
	fuzzyNames: ?[]string,
	# Group dispositions to limit search by.
	dispositionMask: ?[]string,
	# Minimum GID to restrict search too.
	gidMin: ?int,
	# Maximum GID to restrict search too.
	gidMax: ?int,
	# The UUID to restrict search to.
	uuid: ?string,
	# The userdb provider to search on. Must be set to the name of the userdb entrypoint socket. This is necessary in order to support services that implement multiple userdb services on the same socket.
	service: string
) -> (
	# The retrieved group record.
	record: object,
	# If set to true, indicates that the group record is not complete, i.e. that the 'privileged' section has been stripped because the client lacks the privileges to access it.
	incomplete: ?bool
)

# Retrieve membership relationships between users and groups.
# [Supports 'more' flag]
method GetMemberships(
	# The UNIX user name of the user to search for memberships for.
	userName: ?string,
	# The UNIX group name of the group to search for memberships for.
	groupName: ?string,
	# The userdb provider to search on. Must be set to the base name of the userdb entrypoint socket. This is necessary in order to support services that implement multiple userdb services on the same socket.
	service: string
) -> (
	# The UNIX user name of a discovered membership relationship.
	userName: string,
	# The UNIX group name of a discovered membership relationship.
	groupName: string
)

# Error indicating that no matching user or group record was found.
error NoRecordFound()

# Error indicating that the contacted service does not implement the specified service name.
error BadService()

# Error indicating that the backing service currently is not operational and no answer can be provided.
error ServiceNotAvailable()

# Error indicating that there's a user record matching either UID/GID or the user/group name, but not both at the same time.
error ConflictingRecordFound()

# Error indicating that there's a user record matching the primary UID/GID or user/group, but that doesn't match the additional specified matches.
error NonMatchingRecordFound()

# Error indicating that retrieval of user/group records on this service is only supported if either user/group name or UID/GID are specified, but not if nothing is specified.
error EnumerationNotSupported()

# Job management interface for the systemd service manager.
interface io.systemd.Job

# Job type
type JobType(
	start,
	verify_active,
	stop,
	reload,
	reload_or_start,
	restart,
	try_restart,
	try_reload,
	nop
)

# Job state
type JobState(
	waiting,
	running,
	finished
)

# Job result
type JobResult(
	done,
	canceled,
	timeout,
	failed,
	dependency,
	skipped,
	invalid,
	assert,
	unsupported,
	collected,
	once,
	frozen,
	concurrency
)

# A job object
type Job(
	# The numeric job ID
	Id: int,
	# The unit name this job operates on
	Unit: ?string,
	# The job type
	JobType: JobType,
	# Current job state. 'finished' indicates the job has completed; in that case Result is also set.
	State: ?JobState,
	# Job result. Only set once the job has reached the 'finished' state.
	Result: ?JobResult,
	# Activation details describing what triggered this job, as key-value pairs
	ActivationDetails: ?[string]string
)

# List queued jobs
# [Supports 'more' flag]
method List(
	# If non-null, filter by job ID
	id: ?int,
	# If non-null, filter by unit name
	unit: ?string
) -> (
	# The numeric job ID
	Id: int,
	# The unit name this job operates on
	Unit: ?string,
	# The job type
	JobType: JobType,
	# Current job state. 'finished' indicates the job has completed; in that case Result is also set.
	State: ?JobState,
	# Job result. Only set once the job has reached the 'finished' state.
	Result: ?JobResult,
	# Activation details describing what triggered this job, as key-value pairs
	ActivationDetails: ?[string]string
)

# Cancel a specific job
method Cancel(
	# The job ID to cancel
	id: int
) -> ()

# Cancel all pending jobs
method ClearAll() -> ()

# The specified job does not exist
error NoSuchJob(
	parameter: ?string
)


interface io.systemd.ManagedOOM

type ManagedOOMMode(
	auto,
	kill
)

type ControlGroup(
	mode: ManagedOOMMode,
	path: string,
	property: string,
	limit: ?int,
	duration: ?int,
	rules: ?[]string
)

# [Supports 'more' flag]
method SubscribeManagedOOMCGroups() -> (
	cgroups: []ControlGroup
)

error SubscriptionTaken()


interface io.systemd.Manager

type LogTarget(

	kmsg,

	syslog,
	console_prefixed,
	journal_or_kmsg,
	syslog_or_kmsg,
	auto,
	null
)

	continue,
	stop,
	kill
)

type ExecOutputType(
	inherit,
	null,
	tty,
	kmsg,
	kmsg_console,
	journal,
	journal_console,
	socket,
	fd,
	file,
	append,
	truncate,
	broker,
	broker_log
)

type CGroupPressureWatch(
	no,
	yes,
	auto,
	skip
)

type EmergencyAction(
	none,
	exit,
	exit_force,
	reboot,
	reboot_force,
	reboot_immediate,
	poweroff,
	poweroff_force,
	poweroff_immediate,
	soft_reboot,
	soft_reboot_force,
	kexec,
	kexec_force,
	halt,
	halt_force,
	halt_immediate
)

type ManagerContext(
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd-system.conf.html#LogColor=
	ShowStatus: bool,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd-system.conf.html#LogColor=
	LogLevel: LogLevelStruct,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd-system.conf.html#LogColor=
	LogTarget: LogTarget,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd-system.conf.html#ManagerEnvironment=
	Environment: ?[]string,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd-system.conf.html#DefaultStandardOutput=
	DefaultStandardOutput: ExecOutputType,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd-system.conf.html#DefaultStandardError=
	DefaultStandardError: ExecOutputType,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd-system.conf.html#ServiceWatchdogs=
	ServiceWatchdogs: bool,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd-system.conf.html#DefaultTimerAccuracySec=
	DefaultTimerAccuracyUSec: int,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd-system.conf.html#DefaultTimeoutStartSec=
	DefaultTimeoutStartUSec: int,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd-system.conf.html#DefaultTimeoutStopSec=
	DefaultTimeoutStopUSec: int,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd-system.conf.html#DefaultTimeoutAbortSec=
	DefaultTimeoutAbortUSec: int,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd-system.conf.html#DefaultDeviceTimeoutSec=
	DefaultDeviceTimeoutUSec: int,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd-system.conf.html#DefaultRestartSec=
	DefaultRestartUSec: int,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd-system.conf.html#DefaultStartLimit=
	DefaultStartLimit: ?RateLimit,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd-system.conf.html#DefaultIOAccounting=
	DefaultIOAccounting: bool,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd-system.conf.html#DefaultIPAccounting=
	DefaultIPAccounting: bool,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd-system.conf.html#DefaultMemoryAccounting=
	DefaultMemoryAccounting: bool,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd-system.conf.html#DefaultTasksAccounting=
	DefaultTasksAccounting: bool,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd-system.conf.html#DefaultLimitCPU=
	DefaultLimits: ResourceLimitTable,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd-system.conf.html#DefaultTasksMax=
	DefaultTasksMax: int,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd-system.conf.html#DefaultMemoryPressureThresholdUSec=
	DefaultMemoryPressureThresholdUSec: int,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd-system.conf.html#DefaultMemoryPressureWatch=
	DefaultMemoryPressureWatch: CGroupPressureWatch,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd-system.conf.html#DefaultCPUPressureThresholdUSec=
	DefaultCPUPressureThresholdUSec: int,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd-system.conf.html#DefaultCPUPressureWatch=
	DefaultCPUPressureWatch: CGroupPressureWatch,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd-system.conf.html#DefaultIOPressureThresholdUSec=
	DefaultIOPressureThresholdUSec: int,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd-system.conf.html#DefaultIOPressureWatch=
	DefaultIOPressureWatch: CGroupPressureWatch,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd-system.conf.html#RuntimeWatchdogSec=
	RuntimeWatchdogUSec: ?int,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd-system.conf.html#RebootWatchdogSec=
	RebootWatchdogUSec: ?int,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd-system.conf.html#KExecWatchdogSec=
	KExecWatchdogUSec: ?int,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd-system.conf.html#RuntimeWatchdogPreSec=
	RuntimeWatchdogPreUSec: ?int,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd-system.conf.html#RuntimeWatchdogPreGovernor=
	RuntimeWatchdogPreGovernor: ?string,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd-system.conf.html#WatchdogDevice=
	WatchdogDevice: ?string,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd-system.conf.html#TimerSlackNSec=
	TimerSlackNSec: int,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd-system.conf.html#DefaultOOMPolicy=
	DefaultOOMPolicy: OOMPolicy,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd-system.conf.html#DefaultOOMScoreAdjust=
	DefaultOOMScoreAdjust: int,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd-system.conf.html#DefaultRestrictSUIDSGID=
	DefaultRestrictSUIDSGID: bool,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd-system.conf.html#CtrlAltDelBurstAction=
	CtrlAltDelBurstAction: EmergencyAction,
	# The console on which systemd asks for confirmation when spawning processes
	ConfirmSpawn: ?string,
	# Root of the control group hierarchy that the manager is running in
	ControlGroup: ?string,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd-system.conf.html#DefaultMemoryZSwapWriteback=
	DefaultMemoryZSwapWriteback: bool
)

type ManagerRuntime(
	# The version string of the running systemd instance
	Version: string,
	# A short ID string describing the architecture the systemd instance is running on
	Architecture: string,
	# The features that have been enabled and disabled for this build
	Features: string,
	# An array of strings describing the taints applied to the running system
	Taints: ?[]string,
	# The unit search path
	UnitPath: []string,
	# A short ID string describing the virtualization technology the system runs in
	Virtualization: string,
	# A short ID string describing the confidential virtualization technology the system runs in
	ConfidentialVirtualization: string,
	# Timestamp when the firmware first began execution
	FirmwareTimestamp: ?Timestamp,
	# Timestamp when the boot loader first began execution
	LoaderTimestamp: ?Timestamp,
	# Timestamp when the kernel first began execution
	KernelTimestamp: ?Timestamp,
	# Timestamp when the initrd first began execution
	InitRDTimestamp: ?Timestamp,
	# Timestamp when the initrd first began execution
	UserspaceTimestamp: ?Timestamp,
	# Timestamp when the system finished booting up
	FinishTimestamp: ?Timestamp,
	# Timestamp when shutdown began, i.e. when units started to be stopped
	ShutdownStartTimestamp: ?Timestamp,
	# Timestamp when all units finished stopping during shutdown
	ShutdownFinishTimestamp: ?Timestamp,
	# Timestamp when the previous boot began stopping units, restored across a kexec-based live update
	PreviousShutdownStartTimestamp: ?Timestamp,
	# Timestamp when the previous boot finished stopping units, restored across a kexec-based live update
	PreviousShutdownFinishTimestamp: ?Timestamp,
	# Timestamp when systemd-shutdown began execution during the previous boot, restored across a kexec-based live update
	PreviousShutdownLateStartTimestamp: ?Timestamp,
	# Timestamp when systemd-shutdown was about to kexec into the current kernel, restored across a kexec-based live update
	PreviousShutdownLateFinishTimestamp: ?Timestamp,
	# Timestamp when the manager started uploading security policies to the kernel
	SecurityStartTimestamp: ?Timestamp,
	# Timestamp when the manager finished uploading security policies to the kernel
	SecurityFinishTimestamp: ?Timestamp,
	# Timestamp when the manager started executing generators
	GeneratorsStartTimestamp: ?Timestamp,
	# Timestamp when the manager finished executing generators
	GeneratorsFinishTimestamp: ?Timestamp,
	# Timestamp when the manager first started loading units
	UnitsLoadStartTimestamp: ?Timestamp,
	# Timestamp when the manager first finished loading units
	UnitsLoadFinishTimestamp: ?Timestamp,
	# Timestamp when the manager last started loading units
	UnitsLoadTimestamp: ?Timestamp,
	# Timestamp when the manager started uploading security policies to the kernel in the initrd
	InitRDSecurityStartTimestamp: ?Timestamp,
	# Timestamp when the manager finished uploading security policies to the kernel in the initrd
	InitRDSecurityFinishTimestamp: ?Timestamp,
	# Timestamp when the manager started executing generators in the initrd
	InitRDGeneratorsStartTimestamp: ?Timestamp,
	# Timestamp when the manager finished executing generators in the initrd
	InitRDGeneratorsFinishTimestamp: ?Timestamp,
	# Timestamp when the manager first started loading units in the initrd
	InitRDUnitsLoadStartTimestamp: ?Timestamp,
	# Timestamp when the manager first finished loading units in the initrd
	InitRDUnitsLoadFinishTimestamp: ?Timestamp,
	# The amount of unique unit names currently loaded
	NNames: int,
	# The amount of failed units
	NFailedUnits: int,
	# The amount of currently queued jobs
	NJobs: int,
	# The total amount of queued jobs
	NInstalledJobs: int,
	# The total amount of failed jobs
	NFailedJobs: int,
	# IDs of transactions that encountered ordering cycle
	TransactionsWithOrderingCycle: ?[]int,
	# Boot progress as a floating point value between 0.0 and 1.0
	Progress: float,
	# Timestamp when the hardware watchdog was last pinged
	WatchdogLastPingTimestamp: ?Timestamp,
	# Current state of the system
	SystemState: string,
	# Exit code of the manager
	ExitCode: int,
	# Encodes how many soft-reboots were successfully completed
	SoftRebootsCount: int,
	# Encodes how many consecutive kexec reboots were successfully completed since the last full boot. Zero if the Live Update Orchestrator is not available.
	KExecCount: int,
	# Number of successfully completed configuration reloads
	ReloadCount: int
)

type Timestamp(
	# Timestamp in µs in the CLOCK_REALTIME clock (wallclock)
	realtime: ?int,
	# Timestamp in µs in the CLOCK_MONOTONIC clock
	monotonic: ?int
)

type ResourceLimit(
	# The soft resource limit. RLIM_INFINITY is mapped to unset value
	soft: ?int,
	# The hard resource limit. RLIM_INFINITY is mapped to unset value
	hard: ?int
)

type ResourceLimitTable(
	# See setrlimit(2) for details
	CPU: ?ResourceLimit,
	FSIZE: ?ResourceLimit,
	DATA: ?ResourceLimit,
	STACK: ?ResourceLimit,
	CORE: ?ResourceLimit,
	RSS: ?ResourceLimit,
	NOFILE: ?ResourceLimit,
	AS: ?ResourceLimit,
	NPROC: ?ResourceLimit,
	MEMLOCK: ?ResourceLimit,
	LOCKS: ?ResourceLimit,
	SIGPENDING: ?ResourceLimit,
	MSGQUEUE: ?ResourceLimit,
	NICE: ?ResourceLimit,
	RTPRIO: ?ResourceLimit,
	RTTIME: ?ResourceLimit
)

type RateLimit(
	# The ratelimit interval
	intervalUSec: ?int,
	# The ratelimit burst
	burst: ?int
)

type LogLevelStruct(
	# 'console' target log level
	console: string,
	# 'kmsg' target log level
	kmsg: string,
	# 'syslog' target log level
	syslog: string,
	# 'journal' target log level
	journal: string
)

method Describe() -> (
	# Configuration of the manager
	context: ManagerContext,
	# Runtime information of the manager
	runtime: ManagerRuntime
)

# Reexecute the main manager process
method Reexecute() -> ()

# Reload the manager configuration
method Reload() -> ()

# Enqueue all marked jobs
# [Supports 'more' flag]
method EnqueueMarkedJobs() -> (
	# Enqueued unit ID
	unitID: ?string,
	# ID of enqueued job (if successful)
	jobID: ?int,
	# Varlink error ID (on failure)
	error: ?string,
	# Job enqueue error message (on failure)
	errorMessage: ?string
)

# Power off the system
method PowerOff() -> ()

# Reboot the system
method Reboot() -> ()

# Halt the system
method Halt() -> ()

# Reboot the system via kexec
method KExec() -> ()

# Soft-reboot the userspace
method SoftReboot(
	# New root directory for the soft reboot
	root: ?string
) -> ()

error RateLimitReached()


interface io.systemd.Unit

type CGroupDevicePolicy(
	auto,
	closed,
	strict
)

type ManagedOOMMode(
	auto,
	kill
)

type ManagedOOMPreference(
	none,
	avoid,
	omit
)

type CGroupPressureWatch(
	no,
	yes,
	auto,

# An object to represent a unit's conditions

	# The condition type
	type: string,

	# Whether the result of the condition is negated
	negate: bool,
	# The parameter passed to the condition
	parameter: ?string
)

# An object to represent a unit's context
type UnitContext(
	# The unit type
	Type: ?string,
	# The unit ID
	ID: string,
	# The aliases of this unit
	Names: ?[]string,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.unit.html#Description=
	Description: ?string,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.unit.html#Documentation=
	Documentation: ?[]string,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.unit.html#Wants=
	Wants: ?[]string,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.unit.html#WantedBy=
	WantedBy: ?[]string,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.unit.html#Requires=
	Requires: ?[]string,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.unit.html#WantedBy=
	RequiredBy: ?[]string,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.unit.html#Requisite=
	Requisite: ?[]string,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.unit.html#Requisite=
	RequisiteOf: ?[]string,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.unit.html#BindsTo=
	BindsTo: ?[]string,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.unit.html#BindsTo=
	BoundBy: ?[]string,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.unit.html#PartOf=
	PartOf: ?[]string,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.unit.html#PartOf=
	ConsistsOf: ?[]string,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.unit.html#Upholds=
	Upholds: ?[]string,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.unit.html#WantedBy=
	UpheldBy: ?[]string,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.unit.html#Conflicts=
	Conflicts: ?[]string,
	# The ConflictedBy= dependencies of this unit
	ConflictedBy: ?[]string,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.unit.html#Before=
	Before: ?[]string,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.unit.html#Before=
	After: ?[]string,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.unit.html#OnFailure=
	OnFailure: ?[]string,
	# The OnFailureOf= dependencies of this unit
	OnFailureOf: ?[]string,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.unit.html#OnSuccess=
	OnSuccess: ?[]string,
	# The OnSuccessOf= dependencies of this unit
	OnSuccessOf: ?[]string,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.unit.html#PropagatesReloadTo=
	PropagatesReloadTo: ?[]string,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.unit.html#PropagatesReloadTo=
	ReloadPropagatedFrom: ?[]string,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.unit.html#PropagatesStopTo=
	PropagatesStopTo: ?[]string,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.unit.html#PropagatesStopTo=
	StopPropagatedFrom: ?[]string,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.unit.html#JoinsNamespaceOf=
	JoinsNamespaceOf: ?[]string,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.unit.html#RequiresMountsFor=
	RequiresMountsFor: ?[]string,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.unit.html#WantsMountsFor=
	WantsMountsFor: ?[]string,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.unit.html#OnSuccessJobMode=
	OnSuccessJobMode: ?JobMode,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.unit.html#OnSuccessJobMode=
	OnFailureJobMode: ?JobMode,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.unit.html#IgnoreOnIsolate=
	IgnoreOnIsolate: ?bool,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.unit.html#StopWhenUnneeded=
	StopWhenUnneeded: ?bool,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.unit.html#RefuseManualStart=
	RefuseManualStart: ?bool,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.unit.html#RefuseManualStart=
	RefuseManualStop: ?bool,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.unit.html#AllowIsolate=
	AllowIsolate: ?bool,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.unit.html#DefaultDependencies=
	DefaultDependencies: ?bool,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.unit.html#SurviveFinalKillSignal=
	SurviveFinalKillSignal: ?bool,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.unit.html#CollectMode=
	CollectMode: ?CollectMode,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.unit.html#FailureAction=
	FailureAction: ?EmergencyAction,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.unit.html#FailureAction=
	SuccessAction: ?EmergencyAction,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.unit.html#FailureActionExitStatus=
	FailureActionExitStatus: ?int,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.unit.html#FailureActionExitStatus=
	SuccessActionExitStatus: ?int,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.unit.html#JobTimeoutSec=
	JobTimeoutUSec: ?int,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.unit.html#JobTimeoutSec=
	JobRunningTimeoutUSec: ?int,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.unit.html#JobTimeoutAction=
	JobTimeoutAction: ?EmergencyAction,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.unit.html#JobTimeoutAction=
	JobTimeoutRebootArgument: ?string,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.unit.html#StartLimitIntervalSec=interval
	StartLimit: ?RateLimit,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.unit.html#StartLimitIntervalSec=interval
	StartLimitAction: ?EmergencyAction,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.unit.html#RebootArgument=
	RebootArgument: ?string,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.unit.html#SourcePath=
	SourcePath: ?string,
	# The conditions of this unit
	Conditions: ?[]Condition,
	# The asserts of this unit
	Asserts: ?[]Condition,
	# The Triggers= dependencies of this unit
	Triggers: ?[]string,
	# The TriggeredBy= dependencies of this unit
	TriggeredBy: ?[]string,
	# The SELinux context that is used to control access to the unit
	AccessSELinuxContext: ?string,
	# The unit file path this unit was read from
	FragmentPath: ?string,
	# The paths from which drop-in files were read for this unit
	DropInPaths: ?[]string,
	# The unit file preset for this unit
	UnitFilePreset: ?string,
	# Whether this unit is transient
	Transient: ?bool,
	# Whether this unit is perpetual
	Perpetual: ?bool,
	# When true, logs about this unit will be at debug level regardless of other log level settings
	DebugInvocation: ?bool,
	# The cgroup context of the unit
	CGroup: ?CGroupContext,
	# The exec context of the unit
	Exec: ?ExecContext,
	# The kill context of the unit
	Kill: ?KillContext,
	# The service context of the unit (only for .service units)
	Service: ?ServiceContext,
	# The automount context of the unit
	Automount: ?AutomountContext,
	# The mount context of the unit
	Mount: ?MountContext,
	# The path context of the unit
	Path: ?PathContext,
	# The scope context of the unit
	Scope: ?ScopeContext,
	# The socket context of the unit
	Socket: ?SocketContext,
	# The swap context of the unit
	Swap: ?SwapContext,
	# The timer context of the unit
	Timer: ?TimerContext
)

# An object to represent a unit's runtime information
type UnitRuntime(
	# If not empty, the field contains the name of another unit that this unit follows in state
	Following: ?string,
	# Reflects whether the configuration file of this unit has been loaded
	LoadState: string,
	# Reflects whether the unit is currently active or not
	ActiveState: string,
	# Reflects whether the unit is currently frozen or not
	FreezerState: string,
	# Reflect more fine-grained state that is unit-type-specific
	SubState: string,
	# Reflects the install state of the unit file
	UnitFileState: ?string,
	# Timestamp when the firmware first began execution
	StateChangeTimestamp: ?Timestamp,
	# Timestamp when the unit entered active state
	ActiveEnterTimestamp: ?Timestamp,
	# Timestamp when the unit exited active state
	ActiveExitTimestamp: ?Timestamp,
	# Timestamp when the unit entered inactive state
	InactiveEnterTimestamp: ?Timestamp,
	# Timestamp when the unit exited inactive state
	InactiveExitTimestamp: ?Timestamp,
	# Reflects whether the unit can be started or not
	CanStart: bool,
	# Reflects whether the unit can be stopped or not
	CanStop: bool,
	# Reflects whether the unit can be reloaded or not
	CanReload: bool,
	# Reflects whether the unit may be started in isolation mode
	CanIsolate: bool,
	# Returns which unit resources can be cleaned up
	CanClean: ?[]string,
	# Whether the unit supports the freeze operation
	CanFreeze: bool,
	# Whether the unit supports live mounting
	CanLiveMount: bool,
	# The job ID of the job currently scheduled or being executed for this unit, if there is any.
	JobId: ?int,
	# Whether the configuration file this unit is loaded from (i.e. FragmentPath or SourcePath) has changed since the configuration was read and hence whether a configuration reload is recommended
	NeedDaemonReload: bool,
	# Condition result of the last time the configured conditions of this unit were checked
	ConditionResult: bool,
	# Assert result of the last time the configured asserts of this unit were checked
	AssertResult: bool,
	# The last time the configured conditions of the unit have been checked
	ConditionTimestamp: ?Timestamp,
	# The last time the configured asserts of the unit have been checked
	AssertTimestamp: ?Timestamp,
	# Current invocation ID
	InvocationID: ?string,
	# Reflects whether the unit has been marked for reload, restart, etc.
	Markers: ?[]string,
	# Provides details about why a unit was activated
	ActivationDetails: ?[]ActivationDetails,
	# The cgroup runtime of the unit
	CGroup: ?CGroupRuntime,
	# The automount runtime of the unit
	Automount: ?AutomountRuntime,
	# The mount runtime of the unit
	Mount: ?MountRuntime,
	# The path runtime of the unit
	Path: ?PathRuntime,
	# The scope runtime of the unit
	Scope: ?ScopeRuntime,
	# The service runtime of the unit
	Service: ?ServiceRuntime,
	# The socket runtime of the unit
	Socket: ?SocketRuntime,
	# The swap runtime of the unit
	Swap: ?SwapRuntime,
	# The timer runtime of the unit
	Timer: ?TimerRuntime
)

# Configuration and mutable runtime state that can be changed on an existing unit
type UnitProperties(
	# Replace markers, or use '+'/'-' prefixes to add/remove them. An empty array clears all markers.
	Markers: ?[]string
)

# A timestamp object consisting of both CLOCK_REALTIME and CLOCK_MONOTONIC timestamps
type Timestamp(
	# Timestamp in µs in the CLOCK_REALTIME clock (wallclock)
	realtime: ?int,
	# Timestamp in µs in the CLOCK_MONOTONIC clock
	monotonic: ?int
)

type ActivationDetails(

	type: string,
	# Trigger unit name
	name: string

	bootId: ?string

# CGroup context of a unit
type CGroupContext(
	# Slice of the CGroup
	Slice: ?string,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.resource-control.html#CPUWeight=weight
	CPUWeight: ?int,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.resource-control.html#CPUWeight=weight
	StartupCPUWeight: ?int,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.resource-control.html#CPUQuota=
	CPUQuotaPerSecUSec: ?int,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.resource-control.html#CPUQuotaPeriodSec=
	CPUQuotaPeriodUSec: ?int,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.resource-control.html#AllowedCPUs=
	AllowedCPUs: ?[]int,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.resource-control.html#AllowedCPUs=
	StartupAllowedCPUs: ?[]int,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.resource-control.html#CPUSetPartition=
	CPUSetPartition: ?CPUSetPartition,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.resource-control.html#MemoryAccounting=
	MemoryAccounting: ?bool,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.resource-control.html#MemoryMin=bytes,%20MemoryLow=bytes
	MemoryMin: ?int,
	# Relative form of MemoryMin, as a fraction of physical memory, normalized so that 1.0 means 100%. Input only: resolved to absolute bytes, output always uses the absolute form; the two forms may not be combined.
	MemoryMinScale: ?float,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.resource-control.html#MemoryMin=bytes,%20MemoryLow=bytes
	MemoryLow: ?int,
	# Relative form of MemoryLow, as a fraction of physical memory, normalized so that 1.0 means 100%. Input only: resolved to absolute bytes, output always uses the absolute form; the two forms may not be combined.
	MemoryLowScale: ?float,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.resource-control.html#MemoryMin=bytes,%20MemoryLow=bytes
	StartupMemoryLow: ?int,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.resource-control.html#MemoryHigh=bytes
	MemoryHigh: ?int,
	# Relative form of MemoryHigh, as a fraction of physical memory, normalized so that 1.0 means 100%. Input only: resolved to absolute bytes, output always uses the absolute form; the two forms may not be combined.
	MemoryHighScale: ?float,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.resource-control.html#MemoryHigh=bytes
	StartupMemoryHigh: ?int,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.resource-control.html#MemoryMax=bytes
	MemoryMax: ?int,
	# Relative form of MemoryMax, as a fraction of physical memory, normalized so that 1.0 means 100%. Input only: resolved to absolute bytes, output always uses the absolute form; the two forms may not be combined.
	MemoryMaxScale: ?float,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.resource-control.html#MemoryMax=bytes
	StartupMemoryMax: ?int,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.resource-control.html#MemorySwapMax=bytes
	MemorySwapMax: ?int,
	# Relative form of MemorySwapMax, as a fraction of physical memory, normalized so that 1.0 means 100%. Input only: resolved to absolute bytes, output always uses the absolute form; the two forms may not be combined.
	MemorySwapMaxScale: ?float,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.resource-control.html#MemorySwapMax=bytes
	StartupMemorySwapMax: ?int,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.resource-control.html#MemoryZSwapMax=bytes
	MemoryZSwapMax: ?int,
	# Relative form of MemoryZSwapMax, as a fraction of physical memory, normalized so that 1.0 means 100%. Input only: resolved to absolute bytes, output always uses the absolute form; the two forms may not be combined.
	MemoryZSwapMaxScale: ?float,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.resource-control.html#MemoryZSwapMax=bytes
	StartupMemoryZSwapMax: ?int,

# CGroup runtime of a unit
type CGroupRuntime(
	# ID of the CGroup
	ID: ?int,
	# Path of the CGroup
	Path: ?string,
	# The current amount of memory used by the cgroup, in bytes
	MemoryCurrent: ?int,
	# The peak amount of memory used by the cgroup since its creation, in bytes
	MemoryPeak: ?int,
	# The current amount of swap space used by the cgroup, in bytes
	MemorySwapCurrent: ?int,
	# The peak amount of swap space used by the cgroup since its creation, in bytes
	MemorySwapPeak: ?int,
	# The current amount of zswap space used by the cgroup, in bytes
	MemoryZSwapCurrent: ?int,
	# The available memory for the cgroup, in bytes.
	MemoryAvailable: ?int,
	# The effective maximum amount of memory allowed for the cgroup, in bytes
	EffectiveMemoryMax: ?int,
	# The effective high watermark for memory usage by the cgroup, in bytes
	EffectiveMemoryHigh: ?int,
	# Memory NUMA nodes that the cgroup is allowed to use
	EffectiveMemoryNodes: ?[]int,
	# The total CPU usage time in nanoseconds (ns) for the cgroup
	CPUUsageNSec: ?int,
	# NUMA nodes that the cgroup is allowed to use
	EffectiveCPUs: ?[]int,
	# The current number of tasks (i.e., processes or threads) running within the cgroup
	TasksCurrent: ?int,
	# The maximum number of tasks that the cgroup is allowed to run concurrently
	EffectiveTasksMax: ?int,
	# The total number of bytes received by the cgroup's IP stack
	IPIngressBytes: ?int,
	# The total number of incoming packets received by the cgroup's IP stack
	IPIngressPackets: ?int,
	# iThe total number of bytes sent by the cgroup's IP stack
	IPEgressBytes: ?int,
	# The total number of outgoing packets sent by the cgroup's IP stack
	IPEgressPackets: ?int,
	# The total number of bytes read from block devices by the cgroup
	IOReadBytes: ?int,
	# The total number of read operations performed on block devices by the cgroup
	IOReadOperations: ?int,
	# The total number of bytes written to block devices by the cgroup
	IOWriteBytes: ?int,
	# The total number of write operations performed on block devices by the cgroup
	IOWriteOperations: ?int,
	# The number of processes of this unit killed by the kernel OOM killer
	OOMKills: ?int,
	# The number of processes of this unit killed by systemd-oomd
	ManagedOOMKills: ?int
)

type WorkingDirectory(
	# The path to the working directory. Mutually exclusive with 'home'
	path: ?string,
	# If true, use the configured user's home directory as the working directory. Mutually exclusive with 'path'
	home: ?bool,
	# Whether the path to the working directory is allowed to not exist
	missingOK: ?bool
)

	partitionDesignator: string,
	# The mount options for this partition
	options: string
)

	source: string,
	# The mount destination path

	ignoreEnoent: bool,
	# Mount options
	options: []string
)

	source: string,
	# The destination path where to mount the image
	destination: string,
	# Whether failure to find the image is considered fatal
	ignoreEnoent: bool,
	# The mount options to use for the partitions of the image
	mountOptions: ?[]PartitionMountOptions
)

	# The mount options to use for the partitions of the extension image

	context: string

	# The smack process label

	# The hard resource limit. RLIM_INFINITY is mapped to unset value
	hard: ?int

	graceful: bool
)

type ExecCommand(
	# Path
	path: string,
	# Arguments
	arguments: ?[]string,
	# Ignore failure of the command
	ignoreFailure: ?bool,
	# Run with full privileges
	privileged: ?bool,
	# Skip setuid handling
	noSetuid: ?bool,
	# Skip environment variable expansion
	noEnvExpand: ?bool,
	# Run via shell
	viaShell: ?bool
)

type ExecCommandStatus(
	# Process ID
	PID: ProcessId,
	# Timestamp when the process started
	StartTimestamp: ?Timestamp,
	# Timestamp when the process exited
	ExitTimestamp: ?Timestamp,
	# Timestamp when the process was handed off to the executor
	HandoffTimestamp: ?Timestamp,
	# Termination reason (si_code): CLD_EXITED, CLD_KILLED, CLD_DUMPED
	Code: ?int,
	# Exit code or signal number (si_status), meaning depends on Code
	Status: ?int
)

type ExitStatusSet(
	# Exit status codes
	statuses: ?[]int,
	# Signal names
	signals: ?[]string
)

type OpenFile(
	# Path to the file to open
	path: string,
	# Name for the file descriptor
	fileDescriptorName: string,
	# Open file flags
	flags: ?int
)

# Service-specific context
type ServiceContext(
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.service.html#Type=
	Type: ?ServiceType,
	# Index of a file descriptor passed along with the method call to connect to the service's standard input. Only settable at unit creation time via StartTransient(); never set in unit descriptions returned by List().
	StandardInputFileDescriptor: ?int,
	# Index of a file descriptor passed along with the method call to connect to the service's standard output. Only settable at unit creation time via StartTransient().
	StandardOutputFileDescriptor: ?int,
	# Index of a file descriptor passed along with the method call to connect to the service's standard error. Only settable at unit creation time via StartTransient().
	StandardErrorFileDescriptor: ?int,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.service.html#ExitType=
	ExitType: ?ServiceExitType,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.service.html#Restart=
	Restart: ?ServiceRestart,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.service.html#RestartMode=
	RestartMode: ?ServiceRestartMode,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.service.html#PIDFile=
	PIDFile: ?string,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.service.html#RestartSec=
	RestartUSec: ?int,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.service.html#RestartSteps=
	RestartSteps: ?int,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.service.html#RestartMaxDelaySec=
	RestartMaxDelayUSec: ?int,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.service.html#RestartRandomizedDelaySec=
	RestartRandomizedDelayUSec: ?int,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.service.html#TimeoutStartSec=
	TimeoutStartUSec: ?int,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.service.html#TimeoutStopSec=
	TimeoutStopUSec: ?int,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.service.html#TimeoutStartFailureMode=
	TimeoutStartFailureMode: ?ServiceTimeoutFailureMode,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.service.html#TimeoutStopFailureMode=
	TimeoutStopFailureMode: ?ServiceTimeoutFailureMode,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.service.html#RuntimeMaxSec=
	RuntimeMaxUSec: ?int,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.service.html#RuntimeRandomizedExtraSec=
	RuntimeRandomizedExtraUSec: ?int,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.service.html#WatchdogSec=
	WatchdogUSec: ?int,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.service.html#RemainAfterExit=
	RemainAfterExit: ?bool,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.service.html#RootDirectoryStartOnly=
	RootDirectoryStartOnly: ?bool,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.service.html#GuessMainPID=
	GuessMainPID: ?bool,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.service.html#SuccessExitStatus=
	SuccessExitStatus: ?ExitStatusSet,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.service.html#RestartPreventExitStatus=
	RestartPreventExitStatus: ?ExitStatusSet,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.service.html#RestartForceExitStatus=
	RestartForceExitStatus: ?ExitStatusSet,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.service.html#BusName=
	BusName: ?string,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.service.html#NotifyAccess=
	NotifyAccess: ?NotifyAccess,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.service.html#FileDescriptorStoreMax=
	FileDescriptorStoreMax: ?int,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.service.html#FileDescriptorStorePreserve=
	FileDescriptorStorePreserve: ?ExecPreserveMode,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.service.html#USBFunctionDescriptors=
	USBFunctionDescriptors: ?string,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.service.html#USBFunctionStrings=
	USBFunctionStrings: ?string,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.service.html#OOMPolicy=
	OOMPolicy: ?OOMPolicy,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.service.html#OpenFile=
	OpenFile: ?[]OpenFile,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.service.html#ExtraFileDescriptorNames=
	ExtraFileDescriptorNames: ?[]string,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.service.html#ReloadSignal=
	ReloadSignal: ?string,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.service.html#RefreshOnReload=
	RefreshOnReload: ?[]string,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.service.html#ExecCondition=
	ExecCondition: ?[]ExecCommand,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.service.html#ExecStart=
	ExecStart: ?[]ExecCommand,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.service.html#ExecStartPre=
	ExecStartPre: ?[]ExecCommand,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.service.html#ExecStartPost=
	ExecStartPost: ?[]ExecCommand,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.service.html#ExecReload=
	ExecReload: ?[]ExecCommand,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.service.html#ExecReloadPost=
	ExecReloadPost: ?[]ExecCommand,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.service.html#ExecStop=
	ExecStop: ?[]ExecCommand,
	# https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/263/systemd.service.html#ExecStopPost=
	ExecStopPost: ?[]ExecCommand
)

type ServiceRuntime(
	# Main process PID
	MainPID: ?ProcessId,
	# Control process PID
	ControlPID: ?ProcessId,
	# Status text set by the service
	StatusText: ?string,
	# Status errno set by the service
	StatusErrno: ?int,
	# Status D-Bus error set by the service
	StatusBusError: ?string,
	# Status Varlink error set by the service
	StatusVarlinkError: ?string,
	# Result of service operation
	Result: ServiceResult,
	# Result of reload operation
	ReloadResult: ServiceResult,
	# Result of cleaning operation
	CleanResult: ServiceResult,
	# Result of live mount operation
	LiveMountResult: ServiceResult,
	# Number of file descriptors in the store
	NFileDescriptorStore: ?int,
	# Number of restarts
	NRestarts: ?int,
	# Next restart delay
	RestartUSecNext: ?int,
	# Timeout for abort
	TimeoutAbortUSec: ?int,
	# Main process execution status
	ExecMain: ?ExecCommandStatus,
	# Execution status of ExecCondition commands
	ExecConditionStatus: ?[]ExecCommandStatus,
	# Execution status of ExecStartPre commands
	ExecStartPreStatus: ?[]ExecCommandStatus,
	# Execution status of ExecStart commands
	ExecStartStatus: ?[]ExecCommandStatus,
	# Execution status of ExecStartPost commands
	ExecStartPostStatus: ?[]ExecCommandStatus,
	# Execution status of ExecReload commands
	ExecReloadStatus: ?[]ExecCommandStatus,
	# Execution status of ExecReloadPost commands
	ExecReloadPostStatus: ?[]ExecCommandStatus,
	# Execution status of ExecStop commands
	ExecStopStatus: ?[]ExecCommandStatus,
	# Execution status of ExecStopPost commands
	ExecStopPostStatus: ?[]ExecCommandStatus,
	# Reference UID
	UID: ?int,
	# Reference GID
	GID: ?int
)

# A job object (defined in io.systemd.Job)
type Job(
	# The numeric job ID
	Id: int,
	# The unit name this job operates on
	Unit: ?string,
	# The job type
	JobType: JobType,
	# Current job state. 'finished' indicates the job has completed; in that case Result is also set.
	State: ?JobState,
	# Job result. Only set once the job has reached the 'finished' state.
	Result: ?JobResult,
	# Activation details describing what triggered this job, as key-value pairs
	ActivationDetails: ?[string]string
)

# List units
# [Supports 'more' flag]
method List(
	# If non-null the name of a unit.
	name: ?string,
	# If non-null the PID of a unit. Special value 0 means to take pid of the caller.
	pid: ?ProcessId,
	# If non-null the cgroup of a unit
	cgroup: ?string,
	# If non-null the invocation ID of a unit
	invocationID: ?string
) -> (
	# Configuration of the unit
	context: UnitContext,
	# Runtime information of the unit
	runtime: UnitRuntime
)

# Enqueue jobs for one or more units
# [Supports 'more' flag]
method EnqueueJob(
	# Unit names. Jobs are enqueued atomically. Multiple names require 'more' and receive one reply per job. Merged jobs may yield fewer replies; correlate by the unit name in each job.
	names: []string,
	# The job type to enqueue.
	jobType: JobType,
	# Job mode. Defaults to replace.
	mode: ?JobMode,
	# If true, stream job state change notifications. Defaults to false. Requires 'more' and a single unit name.
	notifyJobChanges: ?bool,
	# If true, stream unit runtime notifications on state changes. Defaults to false. Requires 'more' and a single unit name.
	notifyUnitChanges: ?bool
) -> (
	# Unit context. Set in the final reply.
	context: ?UnitContext,
	# Unit runtime state. Set in the final reply and in intermediate streaming notifications when notifyUnitChanges is true.
	runtime: ?UnitRuntime,
	# The job that was enqueued. Always set in the final streaming reply; also included in intermediate streaming notifications when notifyJobChanges is true.
	job: ?Job
)

# Set unit properties
method SetProperties(
	# The name of the unit to operate on.
	name: string,
	# Whether to apply the change ephemerally or persistently. Currently only true is supported.
	runtime: bool,
	# The runtime properties to set.
	properties: UnitProperties
) -> ()

# Create a transient unit and start it
# [Supports 'more' flag]
method StartTransient(
	# Unit context. Must include ID (the unit name). Only the subset of fields settable at creation time is accepted; supplying any other field returns PropertyNotSupported.
	context: UnitContext,
	# Job mode. Defaults to replace.
	mode: ?JobMode,
	# If true and 'more' is set, stream job state change notifications. Defaults to false.
	notifyJobChanges: ?bool,
	# If true and 'more' is set, stream unit runtime notifications on state changes. Defaults to false.
	notifyUnitChanges: ?bool
) -> (
	# Unit context. Set in the final reply.
	context: ?UnitContext,
	# Unit runtime state. Set in the final reply and in intermediate streaming notifications when notifyUnitChanges is true.
	runtime: ?UnitRuntime,
	# The job that was enqueued. Always set in the final streaming reply; also included in intermediate streaming notifications when notifyJobChanges is true.
	job: ?Job
)

# No matching unit found
error NoSuchUnit(
	parameter: ?string
)

# The unit is masked
error UnitMasked()

# Unit is in a fatal error state
error UnitError()

# The named property cannot be set (via SetProperties() or at creation time via StartTransient())
error PropertyNotSupported(
	property: ?string
)

# Job for the unit may only be enqueued by dependencies
error OnlyByDependency()

# A unit that requires D-Bus cannot be started as D-Bus is shutting down
error DBusShuttingDown()

# A unit with this name already exists
error UnitExists()

# This unit type does not support transient units
error UnitTypeNotSupported()

# The unit file content contains invalid settings
error BadUnitSetting()

# The job or unit is already being watched by another streaming connection
error AlreadyBeingWatched()

# The specified process could not be found
error ProcessIdUnknown()


# APIs for querying user and group records.
interface io.systemd.UserDatabase

# Retrieve one or more user records. Look-up is either keyed by UID or user name, or if neither is specified all known records are enumerated.
# [Supports 'more' flag]
method GetUserRecord(
	# The numeric 32bit UNIX UID of the record, if look-up by UID is desired.
	uid: ?int,
	# The UNIX user name of the record, if look-up by name is desired.
	userName: ?string,
	# The userdb provider service to search on. Must be set to the base name of the userdb entrypoint socket. This is necessary in order to support services that implement multiple userdb services on the same socket.
	# Names to search for in a fuzzy fashion.
	fuzzyNames: ?[]string,
	# User dispositions to limit search by.
	dispositionMask: ?[]string,
	# Minimum UID to restrict search too.
	uidMin: ?int,
	# Maximum UID to restrict search too.
	uidMax: ?int,
	# The UUID to restrict search to.
	uuid: ?string,
	# The userdb provider to search on. Must be set to the name of the userdb entrypoint socket. This is necessary in order to support services that implement multiple userdb services on the same socket.
	service: string
) -> (
	# The retrieved user record.
	record: object,
	# If set to true, indicates that the user record is not complete, i.e. that the 'privileged' section has been stripped because the client lacks the privileges to access it.
	incomplete: ?bool
)

# Retrieve one or more group records. Look-up is either keyed by GID or group name, or if neither is specified all known records are enumerated.
# [Supports 'more' flag]
method GetGroupRecord(
	# The numeric 32bit UNIX GID of the record, if look-up by GID is desired.
	gid: ?int,
	# The UNIX group name of the record, if look-up by name is desired.
	groupName: ?string,
	# The userdb provider service to search on. Must be set to the base name of the userdb entrypoint socket. This is necessary in order to support services that implement multiple userdb services on the same socket.
	# Additional names to search for in a fuzzy fashion.
	fuzzyNames: ?[]string,
	# Group dispositions to limit search by.
	dispositionMask: ?[]string,
	# Minimum GID to restrict search too.
	gidMin: ?int,
	# Maximum GID to restrict search too.
	gidMax: ?int,
	# The UUID to restrict search to.
	uuid: ?string,
	# The userdb provider to search on. Must be set to the name of the userdb entrypoint socket. This is necessary in order to support services that implement multiple userdb services on the same socket.
	service: string
) -> (
	# The retrieved group record.
	record: object,
	# If set to true, indicates that the group record is not complete, i.e. that the 'privileged' section has been stripped because the client lacks the privileges to access it.
	incomplete: ?bool
)

# Retrieve membership relationships between users and groups.
# [Supports 'more' flag]
method GetMemberships(
	# The UNIX user name of the user to search for memberships for.
	userName: ?string,
	# The UNIX group name of the group to search for memberships for.
	groupName: ?string,
	# The userdb provider to search on. Must be set to the base name of the userdb entrypoint socket. This is necessary in order to support services that implement multiple userdb services on the same socket.
	service: string
) -> (
	# The UNIX user name of a discovered membership relationship.
	userName: string,
	# The UNIX group name of a discovered membership relationship.
	groupName: string
)

# Error indicating that no matching user or group record was found.
error NoRecordFound()

# Error indicating that the contacted service does not implement the specified service name.
error BadService()

# Error indicating that the backing service currently is not operational and no answer can be provided.
error ServiceNotAvailable()

# Error indicating that there's a user record matching either UID/GID or the user/group name, but not both at the same time.
error ConflictingRecordFound()

# Error indicating that there's a user record matching the primary UID/GID or user/group, but that doesn't match the additional specified matches.
error NonMatchingRecordFound()

# Error indicating that retrieval of user/group records on this service is only supported if either user/group name or UID/GID are specified, but not if nothing is specified.
error EnumerationNotSupported()


# An interface to control basic properties of systemd services.
interface io.systemd.service

# Checks if the service is running.
method Ping() -> ()

# Reloads configuration files.
method Reload(
	# Controls whether interactive authentication (via polkit) shall be allowed. If unspecified defaults to false.
	allowInteractiveAuthentication: ?bool
) -> ()

# Sets the maximum log level.
method SetLogLevel(
	# The maximum log level, using BSD syslog log level integers.
	level: ?int
) -> ()

# Gets the maximum log level.
method GetLogLevel() -> (
	# The current maximum log level, using BSD syslog level integers.
	level: int
)

# Get current environment block.
method GetEnvironment() -> (
	# Returns the current environment block, i.e. the contents of environ[].
	environment: ?[]string
)

# Returned if the environment block is currently not in a valid state.
error InconsistentEnvironment()


# General Varlink service interface
interface org.varlink.service

# Get service meta information
method GetInfo() -> (
	# String identifying the vendor of this service
	vendor: string,
	# String identifying the product implementing this service
	product: string,
	# Version string of this product
	version: string,
	# Web URL pointing to additional information about this service
	url: string,
	# List of interfaces implemented by this service
	interfaces: []string
)

# Get description of an implemented interface in Varlink IDL format
method GetInterfaceDescription(
	# Name of interface to query interface description of
	interface: string
) -> (
	# Interface description in Varlink IDL format
	description: string
)

# Error returned if a method is called on an unknown interface
error InterfaceNotFound(
	# Name of interface that was called but does not exist
	interface: string
)

# Error returned if an unknown method is called on an known interface
error MethodNotFound(
	# Name of method that was called but does not exist
	method: string
)

# Error returned if a method is called that is known but not implemented
error MethodNotImplemented(
	# Name of method that was called but is not implemented
	method: string
)

# Error returned if a method is called with an invalid parameter
error InvalidParameter(
	# Name of the invalid parameter
	parameter: string
)

# General permission error
error PermissionDenied()

# A method was called with the 'more' flag off, but it may only be called with the flag turned on
error ExpectedMore()

##### /run/systemd/userdb/io.systemd.Multiplexer
interface io.systemd

# Local error if a Varlink connection is disconnected (this never crosses the wire and is synthesized locally only).
error Disconnected()

# A method call time-out has been reached (also synthesized locally, does not cross wire)
error TimedOut()

# Some form of protocol error (also synthesized locally, does not cross wire)
error Protocol()

# A generic Linux system error ("errno"s).
error System(
	# The origin of this system error, typically 'linux' to indicate Linux error numbers.
	origin: ?string,
	# The Linux error name, i.e. ENOENT, EHWPOISON or similar.
	errnoName: ?string,
	# The numeric Linux error number. Typically the name is preferable, if specified.
	errno: ?int
)

# The method is implemented, but the requested operation is not supported.
error OperationNotSupported()

# APIs for querying user and group records.
interface io.systemd.UserDatabase

# Retrieve one or more user records. Look-up is either keyed by UID or user name, or if neither is specified all known records are enumerated.
# [Supports 'more' flag]
method GetUserRecord(
	# The numeric 32bit UNIX UID of the record, if look-up by UID is desired.
	uid: ?int,
	# The UNIX user name of the record, if look-up by name is desired.
	userName: ?string,
	# The userdb provider service to search on. Must be set to the base name of the userdb entrypoint socket. This is necessary in order to support services that implement multiple userdb services on the same socket.
	# Names to search for in a fuzzy fashion.
	fuzzyNames: ?[]string,
	# User dispositions to limit search by.
	dispositionMask: ?[]string,
	# Minimum UID to restrict search too.
	uidMin: ?int,
	# Maximum UID to restrict search too.
	uidMax: ?int,
	# The UUID to restrict search to.
	uuid: ?string,
	# The userdb provider to search on. Must be set to the name of the userdb entrypoint socket. This is necessary in order to support services that implement multiple userdb services on the same socket.
	service: string
) -> (
	# The retrieved user record.
	record: object,
	# If set to true, indicates that the user record is not complete, i.e. that the 'privileged' section has been stripped because the client lacks the privileges to access it.
	incomplete: ?bool
)

# Retrieve one or more group records. Look-up is either keyed by GID or group name, or if neither is specified all known records are enumerated.
# [Supports 'more' flag]
method GetGroupRecord(
	# The numeric 32bit UNIX GID of the record, if look-up by GID is desired.
	gid: ?int,
	# The UNIX group name of the record, if look-up by name is desired.
	groupName: ?string,
	# The userdb provider service to search on. Must be set to the base name of the userdb entrypoint socket. This is necessary in order to support services that implement multiple userdb services on the same socket.
	# Additional names to search for in a fuzzy fashion.
	fuzzyNames: ?[]string,
	# Group dispositions to limit search by.
	dispositionMask: ?[]string,
	# Minimum GID to restrict search too.
	gidMin: ?int,
	# Maximum GID to restrict search too.
	gidMax: ?int,
	# The UUID to restrict search to.
	uuid: ?string,
	# The userdb provider to search on. Must be set to the name of the userdb entrypoint socket. This is necessary in order to support services that implement multiple userdb services on the same socket.
	service: string
) -> (
	# The retrieved group record.
	record: object,
	# If set to true, indicates that the group record is not complete, i.e. that the 'privileged' section has been stripped because the client lacks the privileges to access it.
	incomplete: ?bool
)

# Retrieve membership relationships between users and groups.
# [Supports 'more' flag]
method GetMemberships(
	# The UNIX user name of the user to search for memberships for.
	userName: ?string,
	# The UNIX group name of the group to search for memberships for.
	groupName: ?string,
	# The userdb provider to search on. Must be set to the base name of the userdb entrypoint socket. This is necessary in order to support services that implement multiple userdb services on the same socket.
	service: string
) -> (
	# The UNIX user name of a discovered membership relationship.
	userName: string,
	# The UNIX group name of a discovered membership relationship.
	groupName: string
)

# Error indicating that no matching user or group record was found.
error NoRecordFound()

# Error indicating that the contacted service does not implement the specified service name.
error BadService()

# Error indicating that the backing service currently is not operational and no answer can be provided.
error ServiceNotAvailable()

# Error indicating that there's a user record matching either UID/GID or the user/group name, but not both at the same time.
error ConflictingRecordFound()

# Error indicating that there's a user record matching the primary UID/GID or user/group, but that doesn't match the additional specified matches.
error NonMatchingRecordFound()

# Error indicating that retrieval of user/group records on this service is only supported if either user/group name or UID/GID are specified, but not if nothing is specified.
error EnumerationNotSupported()

# Allocate transient UID ranges for user namespace, and assign mounts, cgroups and networking devices to them
interface io.systemd.NamespaceResource

# The type of user range allocation to perform.
type AllocateUserRangeType(
	# Allocate a transient UID/GID range from the dynamic range pool. This is the default.
	managed,
	# Create a user namespace that maps the peer UID/GID to itself instead of allocating a transient UID range.
	self
)

# Assigns a UID range to a client-allocated user namespace that has no UID range assigned so far, and registers it for assignment of other resources.
method AllocateUserRange(
	# The name for the user namespace, a short string that must be fit to be included in a file name and in a user name. This name is included in the user records announced via NSS and is otherwise useful for debugging.
	name: string,
	# Controls whether to mangle the provided name if needed so that it is suitable for naming a user namespace. If true this will shorten the name as necessary or randomize it if that's not sufficient. If null defaults to false.
	mangleName: ?bool,
	# The number of UIDs to assign. Must be 1 or 65536. If type is 'self', must be 1.
	size: int,
	# The target UID inside the user namespace. If not specified defaults to 0. If type is 'self', must be 0 or unset in which case the peer UID is mapped to itself.
	target: ?int,
	# A file descriptor to an allocated userns with no current UID range assignments
	userNamespaceFileDescriptor: int,
	# The type of allocation to perform. If 'managed' (the default), a transient UID/GID range is allocated from the dynamic range pool. If 'self', the peer UID/GID is mapped to itself. Defaults to 'managed'.
	type: ?AllocateUserRangeType,
	# Number of transient 64K container UID/GID ranges to delegate. These are mapped 1:1 into the user namespace and can be used by nested user namespaces for container workloads. Must be between 0 and 16. Defaults to 0.
	delegateContainerRanges: ?int,
	# If true, map the foreign UID range 1:1 into the user namespace.
	mapForeign: ?bool
) -> (
	# The name assigned to the user namespace. (This is particularly interesting in case mangleName was enabled).
	name: ?string
)

# Registers an already initialized user namespace for assignment of resources.
method RegisterUserNamespace(
	# The name for the user namespace, a short string that must be fit to be included in a file name and in a user name, as in AllocateUserRange().
	name: string,
	# Controls whether to mangle the provided name if needed so that it is suitable for naming a user namespace, as in AllocateUserRange()
	mangleName: ?bool,
	# A user namespace file descriptor that is fully initialized
	userNamespaceFileDescriptor: int
) -> (
	# The name assigned to the user namespace
	name: ?string
)

# Adds a mount to a user namespace previously allocated or registered via AllocateUserRange() or RegisterUserNamespace(). This allowlists the mount for access by the user namespace.
method AddMountToUserNamespace(
	# A user namespace file descriptor previously allocated via AllocateUserRange() or registered via RegisterUserNamespace().
	userNamespaceFileDescriptor: int,
	# A mount file descriptor for the mount to allowlist for the specified user namespace.
	mountFileDescriptor: int
) -> ()

# Adds a cgroup to a user namespace previously allocated or registered via AllocateUserRange() or RegisterUserNamespace(). This passes ownership of the referenced cgroup to the user namespace's root user, and ensures the cgroup gets deleted when the user namespace is released.
method AddControlGroupToUserNamespace(
	# A user namespace file descriptor previously allocated via AllocateUserRange() or registered via RegisterUserNamespace().
	userNamespaceFileDescriptor: int,
	# A file descriptor referencing a cgroup to assign to the root user of the specified user namespace.
	controlGroupFileDescriptor: int
) -> ()

# Adds a network interface to a user namespace previously allocated or registered via AllocateUserRange() or RegisterUserNamespace(). This creates a 'veth' or 'tap' device that is assigned to the user namespace, or owned by its root user.
method AddNetworkToUserNamespace(
	# A user namespace file descriptor previously allocated via AllocateUserRange() or registered via RegisterUserNamespace().
	userNamespaceFileDescriptor: int,
	# A network namespace file descriptor to assign the network interface to. Only applies to network interfaces of type 'veth'.
	networkNamespaceFileDescriptor: ?int,
	# The network interface name to use for the network interface inside the network namespace. Only applies to network interfaces of type 'veth'.
	namespaceInterfaceName: ?string,
	# The networking mode, one of 'veth' or 'tap'. If the former is selected a virtual Ethernet link between host and namespace is created. If the latter is selected an Ethernet Tap link is created and a file descriptor is returned for the namespace side.
	mode: string
) -> (
	# The chosen network interface name for the host side of the network.
	hostInterfaceName: string,
	# The chosen network interface name for the namespace side of the network. Only applies to network interfaces of type 'veth'.
	namespaceInterfaceName: ?string,
	# The file descriptor for the namespace side of the network. Only applies to network interfaces of type 'tap'.
	interfaceFileDescriptor: ?int
)

# User namespace delegation via io.systemd.NamespaceResource is not supported.
error UserNamespaceInterfaceNotSupported()

# A user namespace under the specified name exists already.
error NameExists()

# The specified user namespace has been registered before.
error UserNamespaceExists()

# The dynamic UID range is exhausted already.
error DynamicRangeUnavailable()

# The dynamic UID range is not available on this system.
error NoDynamicRange()

# The specified user namespace has not been registered.
error UserNamespaceNotRegistered()

# The specified user namespace has no UID range assigned.
error UserNamespaceWithoutUserRange()

# The per-user namespace limit of cgroups has been reached.
error TooManyControlGroups()

# The specified cgroup has already been added to the user namespace.
error ControlGroupAlreadyAdded()

# The per-user namespace limit of network interfaces has been reached.
error TooManyNetworkInterfaces()

# The specified number of delegations exceeds the maximum allowed.
error TooManyDelegations()

# Metrics APIs
interface io.systemd.Metrics

# An enum representing various metric family types
type MetricFamilyType(
	# A counter metric family type which is a monotonically increasing value
	counter,
	# A gauge metric family type which is a value that can go up and down
	gauge,
	# A string metric family type
	string,
	# An object metric family type whose value is an arbitrary JSON object
	object
)

# Method to get a list of metrics among which the collection of related metrics forms a metric family
# [Requires 'more' flag]
method List() -> (
	# Metric family name, e.g. io.systemd.Manager.unitsByTypeTotal or io.systemd.Manager.unitActiveState
	name: string,
	# Metric object name can be unit name, process name, etc, e.g. dev-hvc0.device
	object: ?string,
	# Metric fields are values to differentiate between different metrics in the same metric family
	fields: ?object,
	# Metric value
	value: any
)

# Method to get the metric families
# [Requires 'more' flag]
method Describe() -> (
	# Metric family name
	name: string,
	# Metric family description
	description: string,
	# Metric family type
	type: MetricFamilyType
)

# No such metric found
error NoSuchMetric()

	# Metric family name, e.g. io.systemd.Manager.unitsByTypeTotal or io.systemd.Manager.unitActiveState
	name: string,
	# Metric object name can be unit name, process name, etc, e.g. dev-hvc0.device
	object: ?string,
	# Metric fields are values to differentiate between different metrics in the same metric family
	fields: ?object,
	# Metric value
	value: any
)

# Method to get the metric families
# [Requires 'more' flag]
method Describe() -> (
	# Metric family name
	name: string,
	# Metric family description
	description: string,
	# Metric family type
	type: MetricFamilyType
)

# No such metric found
error NoSuchMetric()

	# The numeric Linux error number. Typically the name is preferable, if specified.
